aṉṉaiyē! eṉ maṉita uṇarcciyai, ēṉ perumaiyaik kulaitta rōm, uṅkaḷai aṉuppiyatu ēṉ eṉṟu ippōtu terintatu. nīṅkaḷ rōmiṉ uyirukku maṉṟāṭa varavillai. oru rōmaṉ uyirukku-eṉ uyirukku-maṉṟāṭa vantīrkaḷ. avvuyirkku iṉi vilaiyillai. ēṉeṉil ataṉiṉum mikka māṉattaiyē nīṅkaḷ vilaikoṇṭu celkiṟīrkaḷ. uyiraiyum, uyiriṉum iṉiya paḻiyaiyum appaḻiyiṉum mikkatāṉa eṉ naṇpar vālṣiyarukkaḷitta vākkuṟutiyiṉ nilaiyaiyum nīṅkaḷ koṇṭu celkiṟīrkaḷ. nīṅkaḷ celka!
“uṅkaḷ perumaikkum, uṅkaḷ kuṭipperumaikkum, uṅkaḷ nāṭṭup perumaikkum eṉ uyir iraiyāvatāka” eṉṟāṉ.
avaṉ coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ avaḷukkō ēḻai varjīliyāviṟkō viḷaṅkavillai. āṉāl, tam vēṇṭu kōḷai avaṉ ēṟṟāṉ eṉṟu maṭṭum avarkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar.
rōmiṉ pakkam nōkki avarkaḷ kaiyeṭuttuk kumpiṭṭu, ‘kōriyōlaṉas! uṉ peruntaṉmai kaṇṭu rōmiṉ maṉam kuḷirka’ eṉṟu kūṟi annaṟceytiyai rōm makkaḷukku aṟivikka mīṇṭēkiṉar.
6. muṭivu
avarkaḷ celvattait taṉ uyir celvatāka eṇṇip pārttu niṉṟāṉ kōriyōlāṉas. vālṣiyārum ceyva tiṉṉateṉat teriyātu tikaittu niṉṟaṉar. avaṉ coṟkaḷai oruvarāyiṉum muḻumaiyāka uṇaravillaiyāyiṉum, tāy vanta kāriyam veṟṟiyāyiṟṟu eṉṟu maṭṭum kaṇṭaṉar.
uyiraip paṟikoṭuttu ataṉai mīṭṭukkoṇṭu varap pōṉavarkaḷukkākak kāttiruntavarkaḷ pōl kavalaiyuṟṟēṅkiya rōm makkaḷ, tam mātar aṉaivarum valamṉiyāviṉ talaimaiyil miḷvataik kaṇṭaṉar. avaḷ mukattil vīrar kaḷai miḷirntatu. varjīliyāviṭam akkaḷaiyillaiyāyiṉum avaḷ aḻukai ōyntu amainta tōṟṟat tuṭaṉiruntāḷ. ciṟuvaṉ kayasai avaḷ kaiyileṭutta aṇaittu muttamiṭṭuk koṇṭē varukiṟāḷ. avarkaḷ naṭaiyiliruntē makkaḷ, veṟṟi tamateṉa uṇarntaṉar. kiṭṭa vantatum valamṉiyā, ‘rōm vāḻka! kōriyōlāṉas vāḻka!’ eṉṟāḷ. peṇkaḷ aṉaivarum toṭarntu, ‘rōm vāḻka! rōm vāḻka! kōriyōlāṉas vāḻka!’ eṉṟu āravārittaṉar.
itaṉ etiroli pōlak kōṭṭai muḻumaiyumē, uyir vantatu pōl, ‘rōm vāḻka! kōriyōlāṉas vāḻka!’ eṉṟu āravārittiraintatu.
vālṣiyar paṭaivīṭu maṭṭum oḷiyiḻantatu.
kōriyōlāṉas vīramum avaṉ uyirum avaṉ uṭalai viṭṭu viṭai koṇṭu ceṉṟuviṭṭaṉa eṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu.
ciṅkam pōṉṟa iṟumāppuṭaiya avaṉ uruvam ciṟiyatoru muyaleṉa oṭuṅki naṭuṅkiṟṟu.
avaṉ taṉ vāḷai uṟaiyiliṭṭu viṭṭu nērāka āḵpītiyas irukkaiyai aṇuki, avaṉiṭam avvāḷai uṟaiyuṭaṉ koṭuttut ‘tāy nāṭṭiṉ pakaivaṉ, taṉ uṟaviṉar nāṭṭiṉ tuṇaivaṉ āvaṉō? nāṉ uṅkaḷukkum pakaivaṉē! eṉ uyiraik koḷka’ eṉṟāṉ.
avaṉ vīraṉeṉṟu terintatum avaṉ mītuḷḷa poṟāmai oru puṟam; avaṉāl aṭainta ēmāṟṟam orupuṟam āka niṉṟu āḵpītiyasaiyum avaṉ vīrarākiya vālṣiyaraiyum maṉita uṇarcciyaṟṟavarākac ceytu viṭṭatu.
paṭaiyiḻantu pakaiyuṇarcciyum iḻanta avaṉai avarkaḷ avaṉ vāymoḻikkiṇaṅkavē, ‘tāynāṭṭukkup pakaivaṉ, aṇṭiyavarkkum pakaivaṉ, maṉita vakuppukkē oru kōṭārik kāmpu’ eṉak kūvikkoṇṭu avaṉaic cūḻntu vāḷālum īṭṭiyālum kaṭṭaiyālum tākki veṭṭiyum kuttiyum eṟintum aṭittum avaṉaik koṉṟu avaṉuṭalaic citaittaṉar.
piṉ avarkaḷ avaṉ kuṭalai īṭṭiyil kuttik koṭiyākap piṭittukkoṇṭu, rōm mītu taṅkaḷukkuḷḷa cīṟṟamaṉaittaiyum avaṉ mītē kāṭṭi, rōm makkaḷ kaṇkāṇa rōm nakark kōṭṭaiyaic cuṟṟic ceṉṟu, ‘kōriyōlāṉas vīḻka! vālṣiyar pakaivaṉ vīḻka; vañcaka rōmaṉ vīḻka!’ eṉṟu kataṟiṉar.
avaṉ muṭivaṟinta rōmar, tī mitittavar pōlat tuṭittār. varjīliyā attuṇukkuṟu ceyti kātil viḻāmuṉ orē alaṟāy alaṟi vīḻntu māṇṭāḷ. 
valamṉiyāvum, taṉ makaṉai - taṉ vīra makaṉai - naṉṟikeṭṭa annakarukkē palikoṭutta tuyar tāḷātu vāḷāl taṉṉai māyttuk koṇṭāḷ.
vāḻvu nāḷil pakaittuk koṇṭē kōriyōlāṉasai iḻantu tāḻvaṭaintu, nāṇiḻanta naṅkaipōl nalaṉiḻantu māḻkiyatu rōm nakaram.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Caiws Marciws
2.	Vulumnia
3.	Tarquin 
4.	Menenius Agrippa
5.	Volsciaus	
6.	Tullus Aufidus 
7.	Consuls 
8.	Cominus 
9.	Titus Lartius
10.	Junius Brutus
11.	Sicinius Velutus
12.	Virgilia

3. antōṇiyum kiḷayōppātrāvum
Antonio and Cleopatra
katai uṟuppiṉar
āṭavar:
1. antōṇiyō: rōma vīraṉ, oppaṟṟa paṭaittalaivaṉ. āciya nāṭukaḷaiyum ekiptaiyum veṉṟaṭakkiyavaṉ - kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ ārūyirk kātalaṉ. avaḷ kātalāl vīra vāḻvum, araciyal vāḻvum iḻantavaṉ. mūvaruḷ mutalvaṉ.
2. akṭēviyas sīsar: mūvaruḷ oruvaṉ-jūliyas sīsariṉ putalvaṉ-araciyal cūḻcciyil vallavaṉ. maṟṟa iruvaraiyum etirikaḷaiyum vaittuc cokkaṭṭāṉaṭiya talaivaṉ.
3. leppiṭas: mūvaruḷ kaṭaiyāṉavaṉ-akṭēviyasāl antōṇiyiṉ āṟṟalaik kuṟaikka uyarttappaṭṭa peruṅkuṭi makaṉ.  celvaṉ, veḷḷaiyuḷḷamum, peruntaṉmaiyu muṭaiyavaṉ.
4. pāmpi: mūvariṉ potu etiri. kaṭaṟpaṭai valimai yuṭaiyavaṉ. peruntaṉmaiyum, nampikkaiyum uṭaiyavaṉ.
5. eṉōpārpas: kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ amaiccaṉ; paṭait talaivaṉ; araṇmaṉaip paṇiyāḷar talaivaṉ. naṇpaṉ. kātal tōḻaṉ.
6. antōṇiyiṉ tuṇaittalaivaṉ: antōṇiyiṭam paṟṟuṭaiyavaṉāyiṉum tavaṟukaḷaik kaṇṭikka etiriyiṭam ceṉṟu avaṉ perun taṉmaiyiṉāl piṉṉum kaḻivirakkaṅ koṇṭavaṉ.
7. tūtar:
peṇṭir:
1. kiḷiyōppātrā: ekiptu araci. ṭālamikaḷiṉ vaḻi vantavaḷ. aḻaki, oppaṟṟa col tiṟamuṭaiyavaḷ. sīsar, pāmpi mutaliyavaraik kātal tiṟamaiyākak koṇṭu antōṇiyiṉ kātaliṟkut taṉṉait tiṟaiyākkik koṭuttavaḷ.
2. palviyā: antōṇiyiṉ mutal maṉaivi.
3. akṭēviyā: antōṇiyiṉ iraṇṭām maṉaivi.
kataic curukkam
jūliyas sīsarukkup piṉ rōm araciyal mūvar kaiyil cikkiyatu. potumakkaḷ uḷḷaṅ kavarnta vīraṉ antōṇi, jūliyas sīsar makaṉ akṭēviyal sīsar, leppiṭas eṉṟa celvap peruṅkuṭi makaṉ ākiyavarē immūvar, avaruḷ antōṇiyē oppaṟṟa vīraṉātaliṉ kīḻ nāṭukaḷaṉaittaiyum ekiptaiyum veṉṟaṭakkiṉāṉ. āṉāl muṉ sīsaraiyum pāmpiyaiyuṅ kātal cūḻalil tiṟaikoṇṭa araci kiḷiyōppātrā avaṉaiyum kavarntāḷ.
araciyal cūḻcci valla akṭēviyasiṉaic cilanāḷ antōṇiyiṉ mutal maṉaivi palviyā etirttu niṉṟāḷ. avaḷ iṟantapiṉ akṭēviyas antōṇiyai varuvittut taṉ oṉṟuviṭṭa taṅkai akṭōviyāvai avaṉukku maṇantu avaṉaip piṇaittāṉ. piṉ avaṉutaviyāl pāmpi eṉpavaṉait tammuṭaṉ naṭpāyc ceytāṉ.
āṉāl viraivil pāmpi iṟantāṉ. leppiṭasai nayamākak kuṟṟañcāṭṭi akṭēviyas koṉṟāṉ. piṉ antōṇiyum pakai toṭukka muyaṉṟāṉ. kiḷiyōppātrā kātal vāḻvāl, avaṉ ellā nāṭum piṭikkumaḷavum antōṇi acaiyavillai. ekiptai etirtta piṉṉum avaḷ piṇakku vaḻi niṉṟu pōril tōlvikaḷ peṟṟāṉ. iṟutiyil tam kātal vāḻvilēyē aḻunti iruvarum taṟkolai ceytu ekiptai akṭēviyasukku viṭuttaṉar. ulakiyal cūḻcciyil valla akṭēviyaskūṭa antōṇiyiṉ vīrattiṉ arumaiyaiyum ataṉai viḻuṅkiya kātaliṉ perumaiyaiyum pārāṭṭiṉāṉ.
1. veṉṟi vīraṉaiyum veṉṟa melliyalāḷ
kiḷiyōppātrā ekiptiṉ oppaṟṟa araci. avaḷ kariya niṟamuṭaiya vaḷāyiṉum mācu maṟuvaṟṟa vaṭivaḻaki. avaḷ taṉ oru pārvaiyāl ulakaiyē āṭṭi vaikkum tiṟamuṭaiyavaḷ.
makkaḷai aṟiyum nuṇtiṟattilum, avarkaḷai makiḻcciyuḷ āḻttum nayattilum  avaḷukku yārum īṭu aṟṟavar. avaḷ nāttiṟaṉō collun taramuṭaiyataṉṟu. ulakiṉ kaviñarkaḷum nāṭaka āciriyarkaḷum avaḷiṭamiruntē tam kalaittiṟaṉaik kaṭaṉ vāṅkavēṇṭum.
ekiptu nāṭṭu makkaḷ māyattiṟkum mantirattiṟkum pēr pōṉavarkaḷ. avarkaḷkūṭa, avaḷ nāvacaittāl ulakacaiyum eṉṟum, avaḷ muka aḻaku caṟṟuk kuṟaintiruntāl ulakiṉ varalāṟē muṟṟilum māṟiyirukku meṉṟum kūṟuvārām.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyatil viyappu etuvum illai. avaḷ mutalil maṇanta ekiptu aracaṉ ṭālmikkup piṉ, ulakai veṉṟa vīrarkaḷum rōmap peruntalaivarkaḷumāṉa pāmpiyum sīsarum, oruvar piṉ oruvarāka avaḷ kavarcciyuṭpaṭṭu avaḷ kālaṭiyil tuvaṇṭukiṭantaṉar. avarkaḷ ulakiṉar añcum vīramum varalāṟṟil niṉṟu nilavum pukaḻum uṭaiyavarāyiṉum, avaḷ aḻakiṉ muṉ, avaḷ māya miraṭṭalkaḷiṉ muṉ, talaivaṇaṅki niṉṟaṉarēyaṉṟi vēṟāṉārallar.
sīsarukkup piṉ rōmap pēraracait taṉ vīrattālum tōḷ valiyālum āḷun tiṟamuṭaiyavaṉ antōṇi eṉṟa vīraṉē. āyiṉum avaṉ peruntaṉmaiyuṭaiyavaṉ; iṉpa vāḻvilum, naṇparuṭaṉ kūṭik kulāvuvatilum viruppuṭaiyavaṉ; araciyal perumaiyai nāṭiyavaṉ allaṉ. ākavē rōmiṉ araciyal avaṉ kālaṭiyil kiṭappiṉum avaṉ ataṉai ēṟṟup pēraracaṉ ākāmal ataṉaik kaippantu pōl vaittu viḷaiyāṭiṉāṉ.
sīsariṉ makaṉākiya akṭēviyal sīsar antōṇiyiṉ maṉap pōkkaiyum nāṭṭiṉ nilaiyaiyum kaṇṭu, avaṟṟait taṉatu muṟpōkkiṟkuk karuviyākap payaṉpaṭutta eṇṇiṉāṉ. rōmap perumakkaḷuḷ celvākkuṭaiya leppiṭas perumakaṉaiyum ivveṇṇattiṟkēṟpa, avaṉ taṉṉuṭaṉ cērttuk koṇṭāṉ. immūvarum rōmap pēraraciṉ mītu oruṅku āṭci celuttuvatāka uṭaṉpaṭikkai ceytukoṇṭaṉar. rōmap pēraraciṉ peyarāl kīḻnāṭukaḷai veṉṟaṭakkuvatāka antōṇi ēṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ. ataṉpaṭi avaṉ ceṉṟu pārttiyarum, pāktiyarum, pārasīkarum, ayōṉiyarum veruṇṭōṭumpaṭi pōr purintu piṉ ekiptaiyum veṉṟu kīḻ aṭakkiṉāṉ. āyiṉ, antō! nañcūṭṭiya amiḻtākiya kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ vaṉappil īṭupaṭṭu avaṉ taṉ vīramum uyarvum iḻantatōṭu, taṉ veṟṟi viḻāvai aṟavē maṟantu kātal viḻā ayarvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
antōṇikkup palviyā eṉṟa oru maṉaiviyuṇṭu. avaḷ mikunta aṟivum tiṟamaiyum uṭaiyavaḷ āyiṉum, uṇarcci vacappaṭṭa antōṇi pōṉṟa āṭavaṉaik kaṭṭuppaṭuttap pōtiya kavarcciyaṟṟavaḷ. avaṉ uyarvaiyum vīrattaiyum avaḷ naṉkaṟivāḷ. avaṉaiyotta vīraṉaik kaṇavaṉākap peṟṟataṟkāka avaḷ iṟampūtu eytiṉāḷ eṉpatil aiyamillai. āṉāl, avaṉ uḷḷattaik koḷḷai koḷḷa avaḷāl muṭiyavillai. uyarkuṭiyiṟ piṟanta avaḷukku araciyal cūḻccikaḷ nalla pāṭamāyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟai etirkkum eṇṇam kaṇavaṉiṭam illai eṉpatai avaḷ kaṇṭu avaṉ mītu cīṟṟam koṇṭāḷ. itaṉāl kātaluṟavu paṟantatu. avaṉ ceyya vēṇṭum araciyal naṭavaṭikkaikaḷai eṭuttu avaḷ avaṉ araciyal amaiccaṉākac ceyalāṟṟa maṭṭumē avaḷāl muṭintatu.
innilaiyil avaḷ antōṇiyiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāṉ utaviyāl oru ciṟu paṭai cērttu sīsarai etirttup pōriṭṭāḷ. iṅṅaṉam ceytatu uṇmaiyil sīsariṉ vaṉmaiyaik kuṟaippataṟkāka maṭṭumaṉṟu; antōṇiyai rōmiṟkuk koṇṭu varuvataṟkākavumēyām.
kēvalam, peṇṇākiya palviyā iṅṅaṉam āṭavarkaḷai vīrattilum tiṟaṉilum maṭakkuvatu teyvattiṟkup poṟukka villai pōlum! aḵtu inta ikkaṭṭāṉa nilaiyil avaḷait taṉ pakkam aḻaittuk koṇṭatu.
kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ kaṇpārvai eṉṉum nilavoḷiyil avaḷatu karuṅkuḻaleṉṉum mukiliṉiṭaiyē viḷaiyāṭi, ulaka āṭciyaiyum rōm araciyalaiyum maṟantirunta antōṇi, palviyā iṟantatu kēṭṭu varuntuvataṟku māṟākat taṉ iṉpa vāḻviṉ iṭaiyūṟu oṉṟu akaṉṟateṉṟu karuti makiḻntāṉ.
kiḷiyōppātrā ituvarai vīrar, aracar-pēraracar mutaliya palar neñcam tiṟaikoṇṭu avarkaṭku āṭpaṭātu niṉṟavaḷ eṉiṉum, antōṇiyaik kaṇṭatumē avaṉpāl taṉ neñcam taṉṉaiyum mīṟic celvatai uṇarntāḷ. ituvaraiyiṟ piṟar neñcil vīṟuṟa irukkai koṇṭataṉṟit taṉ neñcil piṟarkkiṭantarāta at taiyalar māṇikkam iṉṟu atē neñcil vēṟetaṟkumiṭamiṉṟi avaṉ vaṭivaḻakum paṇpaḻakum vantu niṟaintatai uṇarntāḷ.
ekiptil antōṇi vāḻum vāḻkkaiyaip paṟṟi rōmiṟ palarum palavāṟu tattam maṉam pōṉapaṭi pitaṟṟuvārāyiṉar, avaṟṟaik kēṭṭu mūvar oppantattiṟ kaiyeḻuttiṭṭa maṟṟa iruvarum maṉaṅ kavaṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷukkup potu etiriyākiya pāmpiyiṭam ariya kaṭaṟpaṭai mūṉṟu iruntatu. ataṉāl avarkaḷ eḷitiṟ pēraracu muṟṟum ceṉṟu aṭakkiyāḷa muṭiyātapaṭi avaṉ avarkaḷait taṭaiceytu koṇṭiruntāṉ. antōṇiyiṉ muḻu ottuḻaippiṉṟi avarkaḷāl avaṉai etirttaṭakka muṭiyāteṉṟu aṟintu, avarkaḷ antōṇikku mēṉmēlum tūtarai aṉuppi araciyal vāḻvilīṭupaṭumāṟum, rōm nakar vantu tammuṭaṉ kalakkumāṟum vēṇṭiṉar.
āṉāl, ivvaraciyal tūtarkaḷ varavu antōṇiyiṉ kātal vāḻvākiya naṟuntēṉiṭaiyē attēṉaik kuṭikka voṭṭāmal keṭukka varum īkkaḷiṉ varavu pōṉṟiruntatu. ariya tuyiliḷāḻntavaṉ uṭaliṟ kocukkaṭittāl, avaṉ tūkkattōṭu tūkkamāka ataṉai aṟaintu koṇṟu mīṇṭun tūṅkuvatupōla, avaṉ mutaṉmutal vanta tūtaṉai varavēṟkavō, avaṉ yāriṭamiruntu vantāṉeṉṟu kēṭkavō kūṭac ceyyāmal, paṇiyāṭkaḷ mūlamē avaṉait turattiviṭṭāṉ. itu kēṭṭu sīsar mikavum vekuṇṭā ṉeṉiṉum iṭamum vēḷaiyumaṟintu ataṉai aṭakkik koṇṭu poṟumaiyuṭaṉ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum āḷaṉuppik koṇṭēyiruntāṉ.
2. maṇamum araciyaluṟavukaḷum
‘aṭimēl aṭi aṭittāl ammiyum nakarum’ eṉṟa uṇmaippaṭiyē, palviyāviṉ pirivuc ceytiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa nalluṇarcciyālō, antōṇiyiṉ maṉattiluṅ kūṭac cila nāḷil oruvakaiyāṉa āṇmaiyuṇarcciyum maṉaccāṉṟum eḻuntu avaṉatu vāḻkkaip pōkkai-cōmpal vāḻvaik-kuṟaikūṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. pakaic ciṅkattiṉ uṟumal kēṭṭup peṇ ciṅkattiṉ kuḻaivaiyum kuṭṭikaḷiṉ naṭukkattaiyum pārāmal vīṟiṭṭeḻum ciṅkavēṟṟaip pōla avaṉ kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ kātalākiya paṭṭunūl valaiyai aṟutteṟintu rōmiṟkup puṟappaṭṭāṉ.
antōṇimītu, avaṉ kātal neñciṉmītu tāṉ āṭci celuttiṉum, atē neñciṟku iṉṉoru pakuti vīrappakuti uṇṭeṉṟum, atait tāṉ uṟaṅka vaikka muṭiyumēyaṉṟi oḻikka muṭiyāteṉṟum, atu viḻittu viṭṭāl avaṉait tāṉ aṭakkiyāḷa muṭiyāteṉṟum kiḷiyōppātrā naṉku aṟivāḷ. ippōtu avaṉ vīra uru eṭuttu viṭṭapaṭiyāl avaḷ avaṉ muṉ kāṟṟilāṭum māntaḷireṉa naṭuṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷ āṇaippaṭi, avaḷ cēṭiyar avaḷukku uṭal nalamillaiyeṉṟum, avaḷ ciṉaṅkoṇṭuḷḷāḷ eṉṟum, palavāṟu pēcip pārttum avaṉ pōvatilēyē muṉaintu niṉṟamai kaṇṭu avaṉiṭamiruntu ūṭit taṉ aṟai ceṉṟu, avaṉaip pirinta tuyar poṟātu pitaṟṟi appirivu nāṭkaḷiṉ ovvoru noṭiyaiyum ovvoru ciṟiya ūḻiyāka eṇṇik kaḻittāḷ.
pāmpiyāl alaikkaḻikkappaṭṭu niṉṟa sīsarum leppiṭasum antōṇiyai rōmil kaṇṭatē, kāṭṭil alaintu paciyālum viṭāyālum varuntiya makkaḷ muṉ vēṭacciṟārum ciṟumiyarum iṉmukattuṭaṉ tēṉum tiṉaimāvum ūṉum koṇṭu vantāleppaṭiyō appaṭik kaḷippuṟṟaṉar. ituvarai oruvarukkoruvar ceyta tīmaikaḷaip paṟṟikkūṭa vaṟpuṟuttip pēcātu tōḻarkaḷuṭaṉ kuttalākap pēcumuṟaiyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiviṭṭup piṉ iṇakkamāka oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar kūṭik kulāvalāyiṉar. ivvoṟṟumai neṭunāḷ nilaittiruntālaṉṟit taṉ kāriyaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟa eṉṟaṟinta akṭēviyas sīsar, taṉ naṇparkaḷai antōṇiyiṉ maṉappāṅkaṟintu taṉ oṉṟuviṭṭa uṭaṉpiṟantāḷākiya akṭēviyāvai avaṉ maṇakkumpaṭi tūṇṭac ceytāṉ.
antōṇiyiṉ teḷinta maṉaccāṉṟu ippōtu kiḷiyōppātrāvuṭaṉ tāṉ naṭattiya cōmpal vāḻvai veṟuttatākaliṉ, iṉit tāṉ nal uṇarvuṭaiyavaṉāy āṇmai vāḻvu vāḻa ittakaiya maṇam utavum eṉṟu niṉaittāṉ. itaṟkēṟpa akṭēviyā eḷiya kuṇamum uyarnta kaṭamaiyuṇarvum uṭaiyavaḷāyc cūtuvātaṟṟiruntamaiyāl, avaḷ aṉṟu avaṉatu ippirivu maṉappāṉmaiyil mikavum uyarntavaḷāyk kāṇappaṭṭāḷ. ākavē viraivil av viruvaraiyum sīsar maṇamuṭittu vaittuviṭṭāṉ.
avarkaḷum cilanāṭkaḷ kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ māyavalai yiṉiṉṟum, rōmiṉ araciyal cikkalkaḷiṉiṉṟum vilaki niṉṟu tūya maṇavāḻkkai yilīṭupaṭṭaṉar.
rōmil mūvarum mīṇṭum oruṅku cērntatum iṇakkamaṭaintatum pāmpiyiṉ valimaiyaik kuṟaittaṉa. avarkaḷai iṉi etirppatu aritu eṉṟu kaṇṭu avaṉ avarkaḷuṭaṉ uṟavu koṇṭuviṭa eṇṇiṉāṉ. ataṟku avarkaḷum uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉar. uṭaṉpaṭavē pāmpi taṉṉuṭaṉ kappalil vaittu viruntuṇṇa mūvaraiyum varumāṟu aḻaittāṉ. mukkaṉikaḷum tēṉum pālum ciṟṟuṇṭikaḷum avarkaḷ naṭpiṟkaṟikuṟikaḷāka vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. iṉip pōrāṭṭamiṉṟi nālvarum tammuḷ oruvaraiyoruvar caricamamākak koṇṭu vāḻvateṉṟum, oruvar paṅkil oruvar pukuvatillai eṉṟum uṟuti kūṟiyapiṉ avarkaḷ pirintaṉar.
āṉāl, annāṉku uḷḷaṅkaḷilum oṉṟu maṭṭum innaṭpai oru mēluṟaiyākak koṇṭu uḷḷūra nañcaik kalantu oḷintu vaittuk koṇṭiruntatu. atuvē sīsaratu uḷḷamākum. ulakai muṟṟilum tāṉē āḷavēṇṭumeṉṉum ōr eṇṇattaiyum ataṟkāṉa cūḻccikaḷaiyum tantiraṅ kaḷaiyum allāmal, naṭpu, kātal, nampikkai ākiya ittakaiya eṇṇaṅkaḷ etaṟkum avaṉ atil iṭaṅkoṭukkavillai. ākavē pāmpi, avarkaḷait tam viruntiṉar eṉṟeṇṇik kappalil avarkaḷ taṉṉiṭam vantu cikkiyapōtu avarkaḷaic ciṟaipiṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟiya taṉ tuṇaittalaivaṉ moḻiyaic ciṉantu kaṇṭikkavum, leppiṭasum antōṇiyum tam nalaṉai maṟantu akṭēviyas sīsarukku uyarvum opparavum nalkavum, akṭēviyas oruvaṉ maṭṭum neñcil vañcaṅ koṇṭu avarkaḷaṉaivaraiyum pirittu aḻikkum eṇṇaṅ koṇṭavaṉāyiruntāṉ.
ūḻ ivvakaiyil akṭēviyasukkup pērutavi purintatu. mutalāvatākap pāmpi taṟceyalākat taṉ paṇiyāḷ oruvaṉ kaiyāl kolaiyuṇṭāṉ. ataik kēṭṭatum iṉi leppiṭas utavi vēṇṭuvatillai eṉṟu kaṇṭu akṭēviyas, avaṉ pāmpiyuṭaṉ maṟaivāka eḻuttup pōkkuravu vaittiruntāṉ eṉṟu poyk kuṟṟañ cāṭṭiṉāṉ. akṭēviyasiṭam nampikkai vaittu avaṉai rōmiṉ talaivaṉ eṉṟē koṇṭa peruntaṉmaiyum nāṭṭup paṟṟum mikka leppiṭas, tāṉ taṉ nāṭṭut talaivaṉatu nampikkaiyaiyiḻantatākak kaṇṭatē tāṉākavē taṉnilaiyai viṭṭu avaṉiṭam ciṟaiyāḷiyāka vantu niṉṟāṉ. akṭēviyas apperuntakai leppiṭasaip pārāṭṭuvataṟku māṟāka avaṉ kuṟṟattai vaṟpuṟutti avaṉaik kolai ceytāṉ.
rōmap pēraraciṉ paṅkāḷikaḷuḷ ippōtu akṭēviyasukku etiriyāka mīntiruntavaṉ antōṇi oruvaṉē. taṉatu mūṉṟu etirikaḷilum vīrattālum tiṟaṉālum taṉakkuppēriṭar tarattakka etiri avaṉē eṉpatu akṭēviyasukkut teriyum. ataṉāl avaṉ taṉ taṅkai mūlam avaṉaip piṇittu vaikka eṇṇiṉāṉ. āṉāl ippōtu pāmpiyum leppiṭasum pōṉapiṉ, atuvum avaṉ kiḷiyōp pātrāviṉ valaiyil piṇippuṇṭirukkaiyil avaṉai etirppatu avvaḷavu kaṭumaiyāṉataṉṟu eṉṟuṅ kaṇṭāṉ. antōṇiyum taṉatu maṉaccāṉṟukku iṭaṅkoṭutta aḷavē akṭēviyāvukku iṇakka muṭaiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. uḷḷuṇarcciyum iṉpa viḻaivum ēṟpaṭṭatē illai; avaḷ avaṉukkuk karumpiṉ naṟiya cāṟṟiṉiṉṟu piritteṭukkap peṟṟa veḷḷiya cakkaipōlat tōṉṟiṉāḷ. akṭēviyā ulakameccum maṉaiviyātaṟ kuriyavaḷēyaṉṟit taṉ uḷḷaṅ kavarntavaḷ allaḷ eṉṟu avaṉ kaṇṭu kiḷiyōppātrā irunta iṭam nōkkalāṉāṉ.
3. pirivum, pirintavarkūṭalum
antōṇi taṉṉuṭaṉ iruntapoḻutu avaṉ taṉ kātalukku aṭimai eṉa niṉaittirunta kiḷiyōppātrā, ippōtu uṇmaiyil tāṉē avaṉ kātalukku aṭimai eṉpatai uṇarntāḷ. avaḷ uṭalum uyirum ippōtu iṭaiviṭātu avaṉatu toṭarpu, avaṉ tōṟṟam, avaṉ moḻikaḷ ivaṟṟilēyē nāṭṭamāy niṉṟaṉa. ūṇilum uṭaiyilum āṭal pāṭalkaḷilum avaḷ koṇṭa viruppu aṉaittum antōṇi eṉṉum oru poruḷāl varum iṉpattiṟkuk karuvikaḷāka maṭṭumē ippōtu tōṉṟiṉa. avaḷ cēṭiyār evvaḷavu vēṇṭiṉum avaḷ avaṟṟalō piṟa aracē kāriyaṅkaḷilō maṉañ celuttāmal, antōṇiyaiyē niṉaittu niṉaittup perumūccu viṭṭa vaṇṇam iruntāḷ.
rōmil antōṇi ennilaiyil irukkiṟāṉ eṉṉeṉṉa ceykiṟāṉ eṉpataiyaṟiya, kiḷiyōppātrā maṟaimukamāka oṟṟarai aṉuppiyiruntāḷ. avarkaḷ mūlamāka vanta ceytikaḷ avaḷ maṉattaip piṉṉum mikutiyākak kuḻappiṉa. antōṇi taṉ kātal taḷaiyiṉiṉṟu viṭupaṭṭa taṉṟi, araciyaluḷ muḻu maṉattuṭaṉ īṭupaṭṭu mūvaruṭaṉ ottuḻaittatu avaḷ maṉattai vāḷpōl īrttatu, ataṉpiṉ, avaṉ akṭēviyāvai maṇantāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyaik kēṭkavē, avaḷaṭainta ciṉattiṟkum tuyarukkum ellaiyillai.
acceyti koṇṭu vanta oṟṟaṉaiyē mutalil avaḷ ciṉantu pāyntu kolla iruntāḷ. avaḷ paṇiyāṭkaḷuḷ talaivaṉāṉa eṉōpārpas avaḷukkut tēṟutal kūṟi avaḷai amaitippaṭuttiṉāṉ. antōṇi akṭēviyāvai maṇantatu uṇmaiyil sīsaruṭaṉ avaṉaip piṇaippataṉṟu, muraṇac ceyvatēyām eṉṟu avaṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉāṉ. atōṭu akṭēviyā aḻakaṟṟavaḷ eṉṟum, antōṇiyiṉ uḷḷak kiḷarccikku oru noṭiyum īṭu celuttakkūṭātavaḷ eṉṟum avaṉ kūṟavē, avaḷ taṉ tuyarai maṟantu antōṇiyai mīṇṭum taṉ pakkam iḻukkum vaḻi yāteṉa ārāyalāṉāḷ.
iccamayam akṭēviyā taṉ kaṇavaṉai viṭṭu akaṉṟu taṉ tamaiyaṉaip pārkkac celkiṟāḷ eṉṟu ceyti vantatu.
sīsar ippōtu nēraṭiyāka antōṇiyaik kuṟai kūṟavum, avaṉ naṇparkaḷaic ciṟaipaṭutti oṟukkavum toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. ivaṟṟaik kēṭṭum, leppiṭasiṉiṭam sīsar naṭantukoṇṭa vakaiyai aṟintum antōṇi avaṉmītu cīṟṟaṅkoṇṭu ataṉai akṭēviyāviṉiṭamum kāṭṭiṉāṉ. kaṇavaṉaiyum tamaiyaṉaiyum oruṅkē paṟṟiniṉṟa akṭēviyā tamaiyaṉai nērē ceṉṟu tirutta eṇṇi rōm nakar ceṉṟāḷ.
leppiṭasiṉ naṭpaiyum peruntaṉmaiyaiyum pārāta sīsar antōṇiyiṉ uṟavaiyum taṅkaiyiṉ nalvāḻvaiyumkūṭa eṇṇip pārāṉ eṉpatu etirppārkkat takkatēyaṉṟō? avaṉ taṅkai vāy tiṟakkum muṉṉākavē, avaḷai antōṇi rōmap pēraraciṉ paṅkāḷiyākiya taṉ maṉaiviyeṉṟa muṟaiyiṉ virutukaḷuṭaṉum mēḷatāḷaṅkaḷuṭaṉum aṉuppāmal paṇip peṇ pōlat taṉiyē aṉuppiṉāṉ eṉṟu avaṉmītu cīṟiṉāṉ. antōṇi mītu inta avamatippiṟkākap paḻivāṅkuva teṉakkūṟi, avaṉ taṉ pakaimaikkup putiya cākkuk kaṇṭāṉ. iṅṅaṉam maittuṉar iruvaraiyum oṉṟu cērkkum eṇṇattuṭaṉ vanta akṭēviyā avarkaḷaip pirittu vaikkavē utaviyavaḷ pōlāṉāḷ.
akṭēviyā pōṉatē, antōṇi tāṉ ituvarai valiyuṟutti mēṟkoṇṭirunta ceyaṟkaiyāṇmaiyākiya pōrvaiyaik kiḻittu eṟintuviṭṭu, muṉṉiṉum paṉmaṭaṅku ārvattuṭaṉ viraintu kiḷiyōp pātrāviṭam ceṉṟu cērntāṉ.
“pirintavar kūṭiṉāl pēcavum vēṇṭumō?” nīriṉṟi  veṭitta nilattil maḻaittuḷi viḻuntālaṉṉa avaṉ varavu avaḷ neñciṉ tuṭippaiyum ēkkattaiyum akaṟṟi avaḷai iṉpa ulakēḻiṉum mēlām tuṟakka ulaku eytuvittatu.
tāṉiṉṟi antōṇi vāḻiṉumkūṭa iṉittāṉ antōṇiyiṉṟi vāḻa muṭiyāteṉak kiḷiyōppātrā kaṇṭāḷ. ākavē avaḷ atumutal, eṅkē antōṇi iyaṟkai vīra uru eṭuttut taṉṉaiviṭṭu akaṉṟu viṭuvāṉō eṉṟu añcalāṉāḷ. ataṉ payaṉāka avaṉatu vīra uṇarcci eḻātapaṭi ōyātu kātal uṇarcaciyait tūṇṭi iṭaiviṭā vēlaiyāyiṟṟu. maṟantuṅkūṭa avaṉ akkōṭṭaiyai viṭṭakala eṇṇātapaṭi ceyvatil avaḷ taṉ oppaṟṟa tiṟaṉaiyum māyavittai kaḷaiyum kāṭṭalāṉāḷ.
peṇmaiyē uruveṭuttu vantatupōṉṟa ap peṇṇaracikku itu mikak kaṭumaiyāṉa paṇiyumaṉṟu. kūṭaliṉ uyarnilai ūṭalē eṉpataiyuṇarntu, avaḷ antōṇiyai oru camayam taṉ pakkam īrppatum aṭutta kaṇamē avaṉai veṟuttut taḷḷuvatum pōl naṭittu, avaṉ ārvattaip paṉmaṭaṅku mikaippaṭuttiṉāḷ. avaḷ varavum celavum, avaḷ viruppum veṟuppum, pukaḻcciyum ikaḻcciyum, nakaippum ciṉamum, māṟi māṟi oḷiyum niḻalum pōlavum, uṭalum uyirum pōlavum, pakalum iravum pōlavum niṉṟu, avaṉ vīramum aṟivum avaṉiṭam varuvataṟku ōyvu tarātapaṭi avaṉ maṉattait tākki urimaippaṭuttiṉa.
āyiṉ, eṉṉa iruntālum avaḷum peṇtāṉē! taṉatu immuyaṟciyiṉāl taṉ uyirukkum taṉ kātalaṉ vāḻkkaikkum ulai vaikkiṟōmeṉṟu avaḷ aṟintilaḷ! jūliyas sīsaratu matippiṟkum pāmpiyiṉ accattiṟkum iṭamāṉa antōṇi, taṉ kātal valaippaṭṭu akṭēviyas sīsar pōṉṟa mōḻaiyum pāyum ēḻaiyāy viṭṭāṉ eṉṟu avaḷ aṟintilaḷ!
antōṇi ekiptil kiḷiyōppātrāvuṭaṉum, avar cēṭiyar pēṭiyaruṭaṉum cūtāṭiyum, avaḷuṭaṉ poykai nīrāṭiyum, pūmpantāṭiyum, poṟpaṭaku ūrntum, āṭal pāṭalkaḷ kēḷikkaikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil kalantum nāḷ pōkki vantāṉ. atēcamayam avaṉ naṭaimuṟaikaḷ muṟṟilum oṟṟar mūlam aṟintu vanta akṭēviyas itutāṉ camayameṉṟu kaṇṭu antōṇiyiṉ naṇparkaḷai ovvoruvarāka muṟiyaṭittu aḻittum, avaṉ paṅkiluḷḷa nāṭukaḷai veṉṟu kaikkoṇṭum vantāṉ.  avaṟṟaik kātāṟ kēṭṭum maṉattāl koḷḷāta vaṉāy antōṇi cilanāḷ kaṭattiṉāṉ. piṉ “varaṭṭum, avaṉ eṅkē pōkiṟāṉ. oru kai pārkkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu uṟukki nāḷ pōkkiṉāṉ. taṉ paṭaittalaivarkaḷiṭam, “iṉṟu puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟetirppōm; nāḷai puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟetirppōm” eṉṟu kūṟi muyaṟciyēyiṉṟip palanāḷ kaḻittaṉaṉ. iṅṅaṉamāka ekiptum kīḻ nāṭukaḷum nīṅkalāka ellām sīsar vacamāyiṉa.
4. kātalukku vīram paliyātal
rōmap pēraraciṉ kuḻappattai viṭa ippōtu antōṇiyiṉ maṉattiluḷḷa kuḻappamē mikap peritāyiruntatu. sīsariṉa ciṟumaiyai - avaṉatu naṉṟikoṉṟa taṉattai - avaṉ taṉṉai avamatitta amatippai - eṇṇumpōtellām avaṉ tōḷkaḷ tuṭittaṉa; avaṉ mīcaikaḷ paṭapaṭattaṉa. appōtu avaṉ intak kiḷiyōppātrāvaiyum avaḷatu ekiptaiyum ciṉṉāpiṉṉap paṭuttiviṭuvōmā eṉṟeḻuvāṉ. aṭutta noṭiyē ak kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ uruvam - nakaiyum cīṟṟamum māṟi māṟi vantu naṭaṉamiṭum. avaḷ mukam mayilum nāṇa aṉṉamum piṉṉiṭa naṭantuvarum avaḷatu oyyāra naṭai - tēṉiṉumiṉiya teviṭṭā amirtāṉa avaḷ kuyilicai moḻikaḷ - ākiya ivai avaṉ maṉattiraiyil vantu tavaḻum. appōtu avaṉ taṉ vīrattaiyum taṉ aṟivaiyum iḻantu ‘ivvuruviṉ muṉ rōmap pēraracaiyum eṉ vāḻvaiyuṅ kūṭap palikoṭutta tayaṅkēṉ” eṉpāṉ.
iṟutiyil sīsar maṟṟa nāṭukaḷ aṉaittaiyum veṉṟu taṉṉai etirkka ekiptu nōkki varukiṟāṉ eṉṟu kēḷvip paṭṭatum antōṇi, mēṟpaṇiyāḷaṉ varavu kēṭṭu eḻum kuṭiveṟippaṭṭa paṭaiñaṉ pōl eḻuntāṉ. taṉatu nāṭākiya ekiptukkē iṭaiyūṟu vantatu kaṇṭu kiḷiyōppātrāvum antōṇiyiṉ kātiṟ pukaikku naṟuntūḷiṭum vēlaiyai viṭṭu eḻuntāḷ. viraivil amaiccarkaḷukkum paṭait talaivar kaḷukkum uttaravukaḷ paṟantaṉa. pēṭiyarum cēṭiyarum añci akala niṟka vīraratu pōr avai kūṭik kalantatu.
antōṇiyai viṭṭakala virumpāmal kiḷiyōppātrā tāṉum pōril taṉ paṭaikkut talaiviyāy varavēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉāḷ. antōṇiyiṉ amaiccaṉum naṇpaṉumāṉa eṉōpārpas,  peṇṭir pōrkkaḷam celvatu naṉṟaṉṟu eṉṟum, atilum kiḷiyōppātrā antōṇikkuk kāṟkaṭṭāyiruppāḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. kiḷiyōppātrā itaṉai ēṟkātu avaṉai vāyaṭakkiṉāḷ.
kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ ippiḻaikaḷāl vanta tīṅkaiyaṉṟi vēṟevvakaiyilum veṟṟikkuriya muḻuvaṉmai antōṇi pakkamē iruntatu. avaṉatu paṭai avvaḷavu vīramum naṟpayiṟciyumuṭaiyatu; aḵtu avaṉiṭam niṟainta paṟṟuṭaiyatu; avaṉukkāka uyirai oru poruṭṭāka matiyātu pōriṭum iyalpuṭaiyatu. antōṇiyum oppaṟṟa vīraṉ; taṉ paṭaikaḷai veṟṟimēl veṟṟiyāka niṉṟu naṭattiya nērilāt talaivaṉ; sīsariṉ paṭai antōṇiyiṉ paṭaikku īṭuṭaiyatum vīramuṭaiyatumaṉṟu; sīsarum vīrattilum talaimait tiṟattilum antōṇikku īṭuṭaiyatum vīramuṭaiyatumaṉṟu; sīsarum vīrattilum talaimait tiṟattilum antōṇikku īṭu ākāṉ.
kaṭaṟpaṭai vakaiyil nilaimai itaṟku nērmāṟāṉatu. sīsar paṭai pāmapiyāl payiṟci tarappaṭṭatu; antōṇiyiṉ kappaṟpaṭaiyō ekiptu araci ulāppōkum camayam aḻakiṟkāka uṭaṉcella ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ākavē, nilattaṟ pōr purivatu antōṇikkē nallatu eṉṟum kaṭaliṟ pōr purivatē taṉakku nallatu eṉṟum sīsarukku naṉku pulappaṭṭatu. eṅkē antōṇi taṉṉai nilattil tākkiviṭuvāṉō eṉṟu avaṉukku uḷḷūra accamāyiruntatu.
kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ pōkku itilum sīsarukku naṉmaiyākavē muṭintatu. eṉōpārpas antōṇiyiṉ kaṭaṟpaṭaiyai viṭa nilappaṭaiyē veṟṟiyuṭaṉ pōrpuriyat takkatu eṉṟapōtu, avaḷ avaṉ taṉ nāṭṭiṉ kaṭaṟpaṭaiyaik kuṟai kūṟiṉāṉ eṉakkoṇṭu itilum avaṉ kūṟiyataṟku nērmāṟāka antōṇiyait tūṇṭiṉāḷ. ‘taiyal coṟ kēḷēl’ eṉṉum mūturaiyai mīṟi avaṉum avaḷ colvataiyē mēṟkoṇṭu kaṭalilēyē caṇṭai ceyyumāṟu eṉōpārpasukkuk kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ.
eṉōpārpas kūṟiyapaṭiyē ekiptiyak kaṭaṟpaṭai sīsaratu paṭaiyuṭaṉ cila maṇi nēraṅkūṭa niṟka āṟṟāmal muṟintu piṉṉaṭaintōṭiyatu. kiḷiyōppātrāvum uṭaṉ tāṉē naṭunaṭuṅkit  taṉ kappalaiyum avaṟṟuṭaṉ ōṭumpaṭi āṇaiyiṭṭāḷ; pāvam! tāṉ pōvatu antōṇikku uyir pōvatu pōl irukkum eṉpatai avaḷ maṟantāḷ.
irupaṭaiyum neruṅkip pōrpuriyum vēḷaiyil antōṇiyiṉ kaṇkaḷ taṟceyalākak kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ pakkamākac cāya appōtu avaṉ avaḷ paṭaikaḷ uṭaintōṭuvataiyum avaḷum uṭaṉōṭu vataiyum kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṭatē avaṉ pōrum maṟantāṉ. puviyum maṟantāṉ; pōrvīrar nilaiyaiyum maṟantu, ōṭukiṉṟa tāyppacuvaip piṉpaṟṟum kaṉṟaippōla avaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi ōṭiṉāṉ.
puṟaṅkoṭā vīraṉākiya avaṉ maṉattil kātaṟpēyiruntu uyiriṉum mikka māṉattaiyum uṇṭateṉṉal vēṇṭum!
talaivaṉ pōṉapiṉ paṭai eṉṉa ceyyum? atu nālāpakkamum citaṟi sīsaratu paṭaiyiṉ vāḷukkum, ampumārikkum iraiyākic cīrkulaivuṟṟatu. maṉañ ceṉṟa vaḻiyum pulaṉ ceṉṟa vaḻiyum cellum antēṇiyaip pōlātu ammaṉap pōkkaiyum ulakap pōkkaiyum kāṟṟaiyum pūtaṅkaḷaiyum kūṭat tāṉ karutiya aruṅkāhyat tiṟkut tuṇaiyākum paṭi taṉ kūr aṟivāl iṇaikkam oppaṟṟa cūḻcciyāḷaṉākiya akṭēviyas sīsar aṉṟu ūḻ eṇṇam alaiyiṉ ucciyil mitantu veṟṟi vīraṉāy viḷaṅkiṉāṉ.
5.iṟutip pōr
uṇarcci vacappaṭṭup pōraiviṭṭu ōṭiya antōṇi ekiptu ceṉṟatum taṉ vīrar aṭainta paṭutōlviyaiyum aḻivaiyum kēṭṭu maṉamurukiṉāṉ. appōtutāṉ avaṉukkut tāṉ aṭainta tīṅkiṉ muḻu vaṉmaiyum pulappaṭṭatu. paṭavē iḵtaṉaittiṟkum ētuvāṉa kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ mītu avaṉ cīṟi viḻuntāṉ. aṉṟu avaṉ kaṇkaḷukku avaḷ kuṟṟaṅkaḷ terintaṉa eṉṟu maṭṭumalla; akkuṟṟaṅkaḷ peritākkappaṭṭum, pala tappeṇṇaṅkaḷ cērntum, avaḷ uṭalēpōl avaḷ uḷḷamum avaṉ pārvaikkuk karumaiyākat tōṟṟiṉa. ākavē, avaḷum taṉṉaip pōlavē uṇarcci vacappaṭṭuttāṉ ittaṉai aḻivukaḷaiyum uṇṭu paṇṇiṉāḷ eṉṟu koḷvataṟku māṟāka, avaḷ vēṇṭumeṉṟē taṉnalattiṉāl tūṇṭappeṟṟa sīsarukku uṭantaiyāy niṉṟu taṉṉaik kaviḻkkat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ eṉṟu cīṟiṉāṉ.
iṉmoḻiyāl mukamalarum meṉmalarkaḷākiya naṅkaiyar cevikaḷil vaṉmoḻikaḷ pukuntāl avarkaḷ tuṉpattaik kēṭpāṉēṉ! avaṉatu cīṟṟattāl avaṉ kātalai iḻantu viṭṭōmē eṉṟa accam orupuṟam; avaṉ taṉṉaik kolvatākap pāynteḻumpōtu eṅkē tāṉ avaṉ kātalaiyum uyiraiyum oruṅkē iḻakka nēriṭumō eṉṟa accam orupuṟamāka niṉṟu avaḷai vāṭṭiṉa.
eppaṭiyum taṉ uyiraik kāttuviṭṭāl avaṉ ciṉantaṇittapiṉ avaṉ tappeṇṇaṅkaḷai māṟṟi viṭalām eṉṟu niṉaittu avaḷ ōṭit taṉ irukkaiyaṟaiyiṟ ceṉṟoḷintu koṇṭāḷ.
appuṭiyum antōṇi amaiyāmal taṉṉaik kollat tēṭukiṟāpa eṉṟu  kēṭṭa kiḷiyōppātrā tāṉ iṟantu viṭṭatākavē avaṉiṭam kūṟiviṭumpaṭi āṇaiyiṭṭāḷ. ittantiram muṟṟilum veṟṟi peṟṟatu.
avaḷ iṟantāḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭatu antōṇiyiṉ cīṟṟamellām paṭameṭutta pāmpu mantirattilaṭaṅkuvatu pōla aṭaṅki oṭuṅkiṟṟu. avaḷ ettakaiyavaḷ āyiṉum avaḷillā ulakil taṉakku ettakaiya viruppukkum iṭaṉillaiyeṉṟu kaṇṭu avaṉum cākat tuṇintu viṭṭāṉ. itaṉaiyaṟintum cēṭiyār avaṉait tēṟṟi avaḷ uṇmaiyil iṟakkavillai eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.
avarkaḷ kūṟi vāymūṭumuṉ kiḷiyōppātrāvē nēril vantu avaṉ kālil vīḻntu, “nāṉ taṅkaḷ vāḻvait tolaittavaḷ; taṅkaḷ perumaikkum taṅkaḷ kātalukkum ovvātavaḷ; eṉṉai ataṟkāka eppaṭit taṇṭittālum cari; tāṅkaḷ kāṭṭum veṟuppaiyum cīṟṟattaiyum maṭṭumē kaṇṭu añcukiṟēṉ. taṅkaḷ kātalukkāka uyir viṭuvatāyiṉ makiḻcciyuṭaṉ iṟappēṉ” eṉṟāḷ.
antōṇi avaḷait taṉ nīṇṭa kaikaḷāl vāri eṭuttaṇaittu, ‘eṉ arumporuḷē! niṉ aṉpai nōkka ippēraracum, ivvulakuntāṉ eṉṉa vilaiyuṭaiyatu! niṉ oru puṉmuṟuvalukkāka iṉṉum ēḻumuṟai ivvulakattai veṉṟu veṉṟiḻakka nēriṉum poruṭpaṭuttēṉ. nī kavalai koḷḷa vēṇṭā’’ eṉṟāṉ.
ippōtu kiḷiyōppātrāvukkut taṉ kātal pōyiṟṟeṉa cīṟṟam maṟaintuviṭṭatu; atōṭu antōṇiyō viṟkum taṉ kiḷiyōppātrā pōyiṉaḷ eṉṟa ventuyar akaṉṟatu. muṉpōl avaḷatu matimukamum, tuṇaikkaraṅkaḷum, iṉmoḻikaḷum avaṉukku uyirum ūkkamum aḷittaṉa. avaṉ avaḷ mukattai uṟṟunōkkiya vaṇṇam, “ām, immukattil kātal oḷiyuḷḷavum eṉ uḷḷattil vīra oḷikkuk kuṟaivillai. uṉ peyarāl-uṉ kātaliṉ peyarāl-iṉṉum orumuṟai ivvulakai velvēṉ” eṉṟeḻuntāṉ.
antōṇiyai niḻalpōl toṭarntu uṭaluṟuppuk kaḷō eṉṉumpaṭi māṟupāṭiṉṟi avaṉ karuttaṟintuviya avaṉ vīrarum, tuṇait talaivarum itukāṟum avaṉ kātaṟ perumpuyalālum cīṟṟattālum taṉ nilai iḻantu niṟpatukaṇṭu varuntic ceyaliḻantu niṉṟaṉar. ippōtu avaṉ mukattē vīrakkaḷaiyaik kaṇṭatumē, “iṉit tayakkam vēṇṭā, em talaivar eḻuntār; iṉiyum veṟṟi namatē. ulakam namatē” eṉṟu āravārittu eḻuntaṉar.
maṟunāḷē akṭēviyas paṭai ekiptil vantiṟaṅkiyatu. antōṇiyum avaṉ vīrarkaḷum ataṉuṭaṉ kaikalantu tākkiṉar. akṭēviyas paṭai antōṇiyiṉ paṭaiyaiviṭa evvaḷavō periyatāyiruntum, antōṇiyiṉ āṟṟalukkum avaṉ vīrararkaḷiṉ tuṇiccalukkum āṟṟamāṭṭātu piṉṉiṭaintōṭiṟṟu, sīsar taṉ muḻuttiṟaṉaiyum kāṭṭi avarkaḷait taṉ kaiyāṭkaḷ mūlam cāṭṭaiyāl aṭittu āṭṭuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷaip pōl ellaippuṟattil tiraṭṭic cērkkavillaiyāṉāl, avarkaḷ mīṭka muṭiyātapaṭi citaṟiyē iruppar.
āṇmaimikka antōṇiyiṉ vīrar ivveṟṟiyāl piṉṉum ūkkam aṭaintaṉar. avveṟṟiyait toṭarntu sīsariṉ paṭaikaḷai rōm varaikkum turattiyōṭṭi avaṉai aḻikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu antōṇiyiṉ tuṇait talaivaṉ maṉṟāṭiṉāṉ. āṉāl antōṇiyiṉ iyaṟkaik cōmpalum iṉpaviḻaivum ivviṉṟiyamaiyāk kaṭamaiyaic ceyyak kālantāḻttiṉa. veṟṟiyaik koṇṭāṭum vakaiyil taṉ poruṭkuvai muṟṟilum taṉ vīrararkaḷukku vāri iṟaittum avarkaḷuṭaṉ uṇṭāṭṭayarntum avaṉ poḻutu pōkkiṉāṉ. itaṉaik kaṇṭum taṉ naṉmoḻikaḷai avaṉ puṟakkaṇittāl ciṉaṅkoṇṭum attuṇait talaivaṉ aṉṟiravē avaṉ etiriyiṉ pakkam ceṉṟu cērntu koṇṭāṉ.
icceytiyaik kēṭṭa antōṇi avaṉiṭam eḷḷaḷavum cīṟṟaṅ koḷḷātu maṟṟa vīrarkaḷiṭamum “avaṉaip pōlavē kālanilaik koppa  nīṅkaḷ naṭantu koḷvatē eṉakku viruppam” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. atōṭu sīsar pakkam ceṉṟavaṉ taṉatu āttirattil viṭṭuppōṉa poruḷkaḷai avaṉiṭamē cērkkumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ. uṇmaiyil antōṇiyiṭam nēcam koṇṭu cīṟṟattālēyē etirk kaṭciyil ceṉṟu cērnta attuṇait talaivaṉ itukēṭṭut taṉ naṉṟiyiṉmaiyaiyum taṉ talaivaṉ peruntaṉmaiyum niṉaittuk kaṇṇīrukuttāṉ.
antōṇiyum avaṉ vīrarum irunta nilaimaiyiṉai oṟṟar mūlam aṟinta sīsar taṉ paṭaiyai iravōṭiravākat tiraṭṭik kuṭi mayakkattilum, tuyil mayakkattilum, tammai maṟantirunta antōṇiyiṉ paṭaivīrar mītu pāyntāṉ. koñca nērattiṟkuḷ antōṇiyiṉ paṭai irunta iṭam teriyātu aḻintoḻintatu. eñcila cila vīrarum, antōṇiyum, kiḷiyōppātrāvum arumpāṭupaṭṭut tappiyōṭit tam araṇmaṉai vantu cērntaṉar.
6. muṭivu
vīraruḷḷum tuṇaittalaivaruḷḷum palar iṉṉum antōṇiyai nīṅkātu piṉtoṭarntu vantaṉar. cettālum avaṉuṭaṉ cāvataiviṭat tamakku uyarnta pēṟu vēṟillai eṉṟē avarkaḷ niṉaittaṉar. āṉāl antōṇi taṉṉāl ituvarai avarkaḷ keṭṭatu pōtum eṉṟu niṉaittut taṉ poruṭkuvaiyaṉaittum, tuṇimaṇiyaṉaittum avarkaṭkē koṭuttu “ivaiyaṉaittum uṅkaḷ veṟṟiyāl vanta poruḷkaḷē. eṉ aṟiyāmaiyāl iḻantavai pōka  mītattaiyēṉum uṅkaḷiṭam cērkkiṟēṉ. eṭuttukkoṇṭu naccumaramākiya eṉṉai viṭṭakalvīr! eṉakku nīṅkaḷ ceyyum kaṭamaiyai eṉatu viruppattiṉum ettaṉaiyō maṭaṅku nīṅkaḷ ceytu tīrttu viṭṭapaṭiyāl iṉi uṅkaḷiṭam ettakaiya kaṭamaiyaiyum nāṉ etirpārkkavillai. eṉ peyar colvatē iṉit tīṅku viḷaivikkumātalāl ataṉai maṟantu kālattiṟkēṟpa naṭantu koḷḷuṅkaḷ. eṉakku vēṇṭiya tellām eṉ naṇparkaḷākiya uṅkaḷ nalvāḻvē” eṉṟāṉ.
taṉ veṟṟi nāḷ muḻumaiyum kātalukkāka uyiraip paliyiṭṭa ipperunta takaiyāḷaṉ, tōlviyilum naṭpiṟ kākat taṉṉaip paliyiṭuvataik kaṇṭu vīraṉaivarum kaṇṇī rukuttaṉar. avaṉai viṭṭuccella avarkaḷ evarum virumpa villaiyāyiṉum avaṉ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum vaṟpuṟuttiyataṉ pēril ovvoruvarum piriyā viṭai peṟṟuk kāl muṉṉum maṉam piṉṉum iḻukka avaṉai viṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉar.
akṭēviyas sīsar ippōtu ulakap pēraracaṉāka viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ekiptum avaṉ kālaṭiyil paṭṭuviṭṭatu. āṉāl, avaṉ antōṇiyirukkumaḷavum taṉnilai uṟutiyuṭaittaṉṟu eṉ aṟintu avaṉaiyum avaṉukku uṭantaiyāyirunta kiḷiyōppātrāvaiyum uyiruṭaṉ kaippaṟṟic ciṟaiyuḷ vaikka eṇṇiṉāṉ. āṉāl, iṟakkum taṟuvāyilum ciṅkavēṟṟai makkaḷ aṇuka añcuvatupōla avaṉaiyum kiḷiyōppātrāvaiyum nēril aṇuka añcit tūtar mūlam akṭēviyas avarkaḷ maṉanilaiyaṟintu varumpaṭi ēviṉāṉ.
antōṇi taṉakkup parivu vēṇṭiyatillai eṉṟum taṉ tuṇaivarkaḷaiyum kiḷiyōppātrāvaiyum maṭṭum matippuṭaṉ naṭattiṉāl pōtum eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. piṉ avaṉ kiḷiyōppātrāvai nōkki iṉit taṉṉai viṭṭuviṭṭu sīsarai nayantu nalvāḻvu peṟavatē avaḷukku nallatu; taṉakkum atu maṉattiṟkukantateṉa vēṇṭiṉāṉ.
itukāṟum peṇmaiyiṉ meṉmaiyaiyē kāṭṭiya kiḷiyōppātrā iccoṟ kēṭṭatē araciyiṉ perumitat tōṟṟaṅkoṇṭu talai nimirntu sīsar tūtaṉai nōkki, araci kiḷiyōppātrā antōṇi oruvaṉ muṉ maṭṭumē peṇ āvaḷ; piṟarukku araciyēyāvāḷ, araci eṉṟa nilaiyil vāḻa muṭiyavillaiyāyiṉ araciyākavē cāva aṟivāḷ” eṉṟu kūṟi aṉuppiviṭṭup piṉ antōṇi pakkamākat tirumpi, “eṉ aracē! nīṅkaḷ kūṭa eṉ kātalai iṅṅaṉam paḻikkap poṟukkēṉ, antō! nāṉ kariya ekiptiyariṭaip piṟantu jūliyas sīsar mutaliya palaruṭaṉ uṟavāṭiyatai niṉaittā laṉṟō eṉ antōṇi! eṉṉai ivvaḷavu iḻivāka matikka iṭamēṟpaṭṭatu! eṉ antōṇiyaik kāṇumuṉ nāṉ yātāyiṉum ākuka! anta arumporuḷaik kaṇṭapiṉ, ataṉ kātaliṉ perukkaiyum āḻattaiyum uyarvaiyum aḷaviṭṭaṟinta piṉ, aḵtaṉṟi nāṉ vāḻtal kūṭumō? antōṇiyai otta matayāṉaiyaip piṇitta ikkātaṟ karaṅkaḷ iṉi sīsaraiyotta kuḷḷa nariyaiyum piṇikka muṟpaṭumō? eṉṉa eṉ antōṇi maṉattuṭkoṇṭa karuttiṉ pōkku!” eṉṟāḷ.
atukēṭṭa antōṇi cari, appaṭiyāyiṉ uṉmuṭivu eṉṉa?’ eṉṟāṉ.
avaḷ, ‘’uṅkaḷ uṭaluṭaṉ eṉ uṭalaic cērttu uṅkaḷ uyiruṭaṉ eṉ uyirum uṭaṉ varacceyvatē’ eṉṟāḷ..
avaḷ kātaliṉ uyarvaiyum, ataṉ muḻuvaliyaiyum, ataṉ vīṟṟaiyum aṉṟutāṉ avaḷ kaṇṭāṉ.
avaṉ mukam ellaiyillāta pēriṉpak katir vīci oḷirntatu. avvoḷi kaṇṭu kiḷiyōppātrāvum kātaṟ kaṭaliṉ karai kaṇṭavaḷāṉāḷ. avaḷ “iṉip piṟappu iṟappum iṉpatuṉpamum māṟi māṟi alaipōl vantu vantu mōtum  ivvāḻkkaik kaṭalaik kaṇṭu nāṉ malaivaṭaiyēṉ. eṉ antōṇiyē ariya antōṇiyākak koṇṭu ataṉaik kaṭappēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉait taḻuvi muttamiṭṭāḷ.
vāḻkkai muṟṟilum piṟar aṭaiyum iṉpattiṉum avvoru noṭiyil avarkaḷ aṭainta iṉpam periteṉṉalām.
antōṇi kiḷiyōppātrāvai aṉṟu maṇanta maṇamakaḷiṉum uyarvāka eṭuttuṇaittu muṭinīvi, “iṟaivaṉ uṉṉaik kākka! uṉṉāl piṟaviyiṉ payaṉ peṟṟuviṭṭa eṉakku iṉi eṉṉa kuṟai? uṉ kātal niṟaivuṭaṉ māḷakkiṭṭiya immāḷvu vāḻviṉum niṟaivuṭaiyatu! iṉa nāṉ rōmaṉāka niṉṟu uyirviṭa vēṇṭum. nīyum ṭālamikaḷiṉ vaḻittōṉṟal eṉṟa takutikkum rōmaṉ kātali eṉṟa takutikkum iṇaṅka niṟaivāḻveytuvāy! kātaṟ kaṭavuḷiṉ pātukāppukku uṉṉai viṭuttuc celkiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi avaḷai viṭṭakaṉṟu veḷiyiṭam ceṉṟu taṉ vāḷālēyē taṉṉai māyttuk koṇṭāṉ.
avaṉ iṟantatu kēṭṭa kiḷiyōppātrā uyiriḻanta uṭalpōl ceyalaṟṟu niṉṟāḷ. piṉ tēṟit taṉ kātaṟ kaṭaṉāṟṟa eṇṇumaḷavil sīsariṉ tūtaṉ vantu palavakai iṉiya moḻikaḷāl  avaḷai vayappaṭutta muyaṉṟāṉ. avaḷ avaṉai acaṭṭaiyākap pārttut taṉ iṟuti viruppaṅkaḷai sīsarukku uṇarttu muṟaiyil avaṉiṭam kūṟiṉāḷ:
“eṉakku sīsar rivātāyiṉ ataṉai eṉ putalvaṉiṭamum, eṉ kātalaṉ naṇpariṭamum kāṭṭuka! “
“nāṉ antōṇiyiṉ uyirē uyirāy avaṉ vāḻvē vāḻvāy vāḻntavaḷ; atupōl avaṉ māḷavē māḷvāy māḷat tuṇintu viṭṭēṉ. sīsar nāṭiya rōmap pēraracaiyum, sīsar nāṭiya ekiptaiyum iḻappatil eṉakku varuttamillai. antōṇi illā ulakai sīsar āḷaṭṭum. nāṉ antōṇi yaiyum avaṉ vāḻum ulakaiyumē āḷap pōkiṟēṉ.
“kātal taḷaiyāl kaṭṭuṇṭirunta ciṅkattaip piṉpaṟṟi nāṉ collukiṟēṉ. ciṟumaippaṭṭa cūḻcci valaiyāl ulakaik kavarnta sīsar ivveṟṟulakai āḷaṭṭum.”
ivvāṟu perumitattuṭaṉ kūṟiviṭṭu avaḷ taṉṉiṭamē oru ciṟu peṭṭiyil karantuvaittirunta koṭiya naccuppampu oṉṟaiyeṭuttu ‘’ ‘itukāṟum piḷḷaipōl uṉṉai vaḷarttēṉ. ippōtu naṉṟi maṟavātu eṉ kātaṟkaṉiyuṭaṉ eṉṉaic cērppippāy’ eṉṟu kūṟi, ataṉ nañcai uṭalil ēṟṟāḷ. arai noṭiyil antōṇiyiṉ uṭaluṭaṉ avaḷ uṭal kiṭappa uyir antōṇiyai nāṭic ceṉṟatu.
iṟutiyil akṭēviyas sīsar vantu kātalaracaṉum kātalaraciyumāy māḷvilum paṇiyātu vīṟuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiya attuṇaivaraik kaṇṭāṉ. oru noṭi avaṉ ulakiyal aṟivukūṭak kalaṅkiyatu. tamatu tannalattālum rōmiṉ cīrkulaivālum itukāṟum iṟanta oppaṟṟa rōmat talaivarkaḷai eṇṇip pārttāṉ. “vīrattukku iraiyākiya jūliyas sīsareṉṉa, oḻukkattiṉ uyarkuṉṟākiya purūṭṭa seṉṉa, naṭpiṉ aṇikalamākiya kāsiyeṉṉa,  kaṭaiciyil kātaliṉ oppaṟṟa cikaramākiya ivvīra antōṇiyeṉṉa, ipperuntakai rōma vīrar aṉaivarum māṇṭaṉarē! rōmiṉ peruvāḻviṉ ūṟṟukaḷāṉa ivarkaḷ pōṉapiṉ pālaiyāy viṭṭa ippuṉmai ulakō ippāviyēṉukkuk koṭuttu vaittu!” eṉṟu maṉamāḻkik kaṇṇīrukuttāṉ.
ulakap pēraracai veṉṟa veṟṟivīraṉatu veṟṟiyaic ciṟumaip paṭuttiṉa kātaṟ pēraracaṉum pēraraciyum ākiya av iruvaruṭaiya   uyartaṉik kuṇaṅkaḷai eṇṇi eṇṇi ekiptiyarum rōmarum āṟṟoṇāt tuyareytiṉar.
4. naṉku muṭivuṟiṉ nalamēyaṉaittum
(All is well that ends well)
katai uṟuppiṉar
āṭavar:
1.  korārṭu: ariya maruttuvaṉ-heleṉā tantai.
2. perṭṭiram: kālañ ceṉṟa rūsilāṉ perumakaṉ putalvaṉ-heleṉāviṉ kātaluk kaḷāy avaḷai veṟuttum iṟutiyil maṇantu ēṟṟukkoṇṭāṉ.
3. pirāṉcu aracaṉ: kālañ ceṉṟa rūsilāṉ perumakaṉiṭam paṟṟu koṇṭavaṉ -heleṉāvaiyum perṭṭiramaiyum maṇaviṉaiyāl iṇaittavaṉ.
peṇṭir:
1. rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi: rūsilāṉ perumakaṉ maṉaivi-perṭṭirām tāy-heleṉāviṉ kātalukkut tuṇai tantavaḷ.
2. heleṉā: maruttuvaṉ korārṭu makaḷ-tantai iṟantapiṉ, rūsilāṉ peruṅkuṭiyil paṇiyāḷāy perṭṭirāmaik kātalittu aracaṉutaviyāl maṇantāḷ.
3. tayāṉā: piḷāreṉcu nakariluḷḷa naṅkai-heleṉāviṉ ārūyirt tōḻiyāṉavaḷ-perṭṭiram takāk kātalukkāḷāy ataṉai maṟuttavaḷ.
4. mūtāṭṭi: tayāṉāviṉ tāy.
kataic curukkam
korārṭu ariya maruttuvaṉ. avaṉ iṟantapiṉ avaṉ makaḷ heleṉā rūsilāṉ peruṅkuṭiyil paṇiyāḷāy amarntāḷ. pirāṉcu aracaṉ naṇpaṉākiya rūsilāṉ perumakaṉ iṟantapiṉ avaṉ makaṉ perṭṭiramai aracaṉ amaiccar mūlam aḻaittut taṉṉiṭam vaittuk koṇṭāṉ. avaṉiṭam uḷḷūṟak kātalkoṇṭa heleṉā, appakaiyil rūsilāṉ  perumāṭṭiyiṉ tuṇaipeṟṟu aracaṉiṭam ceṉṟu avaṉatu pāṇṭu nōyait tantaiyiṉ arumaruntoṉṟāl kuṇap paṭuttiṉāḷ. aracaṉ  makiḻcciyuṟṟu avaḷukkut taṉ kaṇaiyāḻiyaik koṭuttatuṭaṉ avaḷ virumpiya āṭavaṉai maṇa muṭippatākac colla, perṭṭiramaiyē virumpi maṇa muṭittāḷ. perṭṭiramaiyē virumpi maṇa muṭittāḷ. poṭṭiram avaḷai veṟuttu rūsilāṉa viṭṭuviṭṭup piḷāreṉcu ceṉṟu, aṅkē paṭait talaivaṉāyt tayāṉā eṉṟa peṇṇaik kātalittāṉ.
ciṉṉāḷil maṉa veṟuppuṟṟa heleṉāvum veḷiyēkit tirintu, iṟutiyil taṟceyalāy atē piḷāreṉcil vantu tayāṉāviṉ ārūyirt tōḻiyāy avaḷ tuṇaiyāl avaḷuṭaiyil perṭṭiramuṭaṉ taṉayiṭamaṭaintu, avaṉ kātalaip peṟṟu, avaṉ kaṇaiyāḻiyai vāṅkik koṇṭāḷ. itaṟkiṭaiyil rūsilāṉukkuc ceṉṟu maṇa makkaḷ pirivu kēṭṭu varuntiya aracaṉ, perṭṭiramaiyaḻaittu heleṉā paṟṟi ucāvi, avaṉ avaḷai veṟuttu vēṟu peṇṇaik kaḷavu muṟaiyil maṇantatākak kūṟiṉāṉ. atēcamayam maṟaiviliruntu heleṉāvum tayāṉāvum vantu kaṇaiyāḻi kāṭṭip perṭṭiramiṉ maṉattai māṟṟiṉar. perṭṭiram heleṉāvuṭaṉ vāḻntāḷ. aracaṉ utaviyāl tayāṉā iṉṉoru perumakaṉai maṇantāḷ.
1.  kaikkiḷaik kātal
pirāṉcu nāṭṭil 1korārṭu eṉṟa ōr ariya maruttuvaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ nōy ārāycciyilum maruntārāycciyilum oppaṟṟavaṉ. annāḷaiya maruttuvaraṟivukku appāṟpaṭṭa pala putiya maruntu vakaikaḷ avaṉukkut terintiruntaṉa.
korārṭukku 2heleṉā eṉṉum putalvi orutti iruntāḷ. korārṭu avaḷukku ettakaiya poruṭkuvaiyum vaikkavillai. āyiṉum avaṉ taṉ maruntu vakaikaḷaiyum avaṟṟai vaḻaṅkum muṟaikaḷaiyu maṭṭum avaḷukkuk kaṟpittiruntāṉ.
tantai iṟantapiṉ heleṉā taṉ tantaikku aṟimukamāṉa 3rūsilāṉ eṉṟa peruṅkuṭiyiṟ ceṉṟu, paṇiyāhḷay amarntiruntāḷ. ap peruṅkuṭiyiṉ peruntalaivaṉāyirunta paḻaiya rūsilāṉ perumakaṉār iṟantuviṭṭamaiyāl, iḷaiñaṉākiya avar makaṉ 4perṭṭiram putiya rūsilāṉ perumakaṉ āṉāṉ.
paḻaiya rūsilāṉ perumakaṉār pirāṉcu aracaṉukku neruṅkiya naṇpar. ākavē, aracaṉ avar makaṉākiya perṭṭiramaik kāṇa virumpi avaṉai aḻaittuvarum poruṭṭut taṉ amaiccaṉāṉa 5lāpiyū eṉpavaṉai, avaṉpāl aṉuppiṉāṉ. perṭṭiram avvamaiccaṉuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭu aracaṉaik kāṇacceṉṟāṉ.
rūsilāṉ kuṭiyil kālavaṭi vaittu mutal heleṉātaṉ neñcai muṟṟilum perṭṭiramukku paṟikoṭuttāḷ. āyiṉum, taṉ eḻaimaiyum tuṇaiyaṟṟa taṉmaiyum eṅkē, avaṉ uyarkuṭiyum pukaḻum eṅkē, eṉṟu avaḷ maṉam cōmpiṉāḷ.
perṭṭirāmiṉ tāyāṉa rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi heleṉāviṉ maṉanilaiyai uyttuṇarntu koṇṭāḷ. avaḷ pārvaikku heleṉāviṉ ēḻaimaiyai viṭa avaḷ aḻakum kuṇamumē viḷakkamākat tōṉṟiṉa. ākavē avaḷ heleṉāviṉ kātalukku iṇakka maḷittāḷ. āyiṉum, perṭṭiram avaḷai matiyāmal acaṭṭaiyāyiruntāṉ. ataṉāl avaḷ mikavum maṉaccōrvu aṭaiya nērntatu.
perṭṭiram pōṉapōtu heleṉā, avaṉ pōṉa pakkamē nōkki niṉṟu, “antō neñcārak kalantu uṟavāṭāviṭiṉum ituvaraiyiṟ kaṇṇāravēṇuṅ kāṇa muṭintatu; iṉṟu ataṟkum vaḻiyillaiyē” eṉṟu maṉa māḻkiṉāḷ.
perṭṭiramai aḻaittup pōka vanta amaiccaṉ aracavaik kāriyaṅkaḷ palavum pēcikkoṇṭiruntapōtu, aracaṉ pāṇṭu nōyiṉāl varuntukiṉṟāṉeṉṟum, ataṟku araṇmaṉai maruttuvar aṉaivarum paṟpala maruntukaḷ koṭuttum kuṇam vantapāṭillai eṉṟum kūṟiyatu avaḷ niṉaiviṟku vantatu. varavē, avaḷ maṉattil cila āḻnta eṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. “ā! eṉ tantai kuṟittut tantu pōṉa putuvakai maruntukaḷuḷ pāṇṭu nōykkāṉa maruntum oṉṟaṉṟō? ataṉ mūlam oruvēḷai eṉ eṇṇaṅkaḷ īṭēṟumāyiṉ evvaḷavu ciṟappākum” eṉṟa avaḷ vāyviṭṭuk kūṟiṉāḷ.
avaḷ kūṟiya moḻikaḷai, aṭuttu niṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭirunta rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi, “aracaṉ piṇiyum, im maṅkai piṇiyum oruṅkē nīṅkutal tiruvuḷamāyiṉ, aḵtu aṅṅaṉamē niṟaivēṟuka” eṉṟu maṉattuṭkoṇṭu, avaḷai aracaṉpāl viṭuttaṉaḷ.
heleṉā, aracaṉai aṇukuvatē mutalil arumaiyāka iruntatu. aṇukiyapōtum avaḷ maruntai aracaṉ uṭkoḷḷuvataṟku avaṉ avaiyōr oruppaṭavillai. innilaiyil, aracaṉ kātali icceyti viḻuntatu. taṉatu nalviṉaip payaṉāl tūṇṭappaṭṭu avaṉ, “eppaṭiyum nōy kuṇamaṭaivatākak kāṇavillai. piṟa maruttuvar aṉaivarum kaiviṭṭaṉar. ipputu maruntālkuṇam ēṟpaṭāviṭṭālum kēṭeṉṉa? aḵtu oru vēḷai eṉ uyir koḷḷum nañcāy iruntāluṅkūṭa ivvēḷaikku aḵtu eṉṉai nōyiṉiṉṟum tappa vaikkum karuviyākavē amaiyum” eṉṟu kūṟi avaḷai varaviṭuttāṉ. piṉpu avaḷ korārṭiṉ putalvi eṉṟum maruntu korāṭṭiṉ putu maruntukaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉṟum kēṭṭapōtu aracaṉukkup piṉṉum nampikkai uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭiyum heleṉāvum kaṇṭa kaṉavu naṉavāyiṟṟu. ammaruntu uṭkoṇṭa iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷil aracaṉ nōyiṉ aṭaiyāḷamē illātapaṭi muḻumaiyum kuṇamaṭaintāṉ. ākavē, avaṉ maṭṭaṟṟa makiḻcci koṇṭu avaḷukku vilai matippaṟṟa oru kaṇaiyāḻiyaip paricāka vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. atōṭu avavaiyil yārai vēṇṭumāṉālum avaḷ maṇantukoḷḷalām eṉṟum, avaḷukku avaṉ uṟuti koṭuttāṉ. heleṉā, aṅkē vēṟu yārmītum kaṇṇeṭuttup pārāmal rūsilāṉ peru makaṉākiya perṭṭiramaiyē kaṇavaṉākat terinteṭuttāḷ.
perṭṭiram, paḻakka mikutiyiṉālum paṇiyāḷ eṉṟa puṟakkaṇippiṉālum heleṉāviṉ aḻakaiyum, uyar kuṇaṅkaḷaiyum matiyātavaṉāyiruntāṉ. ākavē, taṉ viruppattiṟku māṟāka avaḷ taṉ mītu cumattappaṭṭatai avaṉ veṟuttāṉ. āyiṉum eṉ ceyvatu? aracaṉ āṇaiyai maṟukka muṭiyātu; ātalāl, perṭṭiram avaḷai vēṇṭā veṟuppāka maṇantu koṇṭāṉ.
āyiṉum, maṇattaittāṉ oruvar valintu cumatta muṭiyumēyaṉṟik kātalaic cumatta muṭiyātaṉṟō? heleṉā, ‘kātalaṟṟa maṇam’ eṉṉum kaṭu narakiṟku āḷāṉāḷ. maṇa nāḷilēyē perṭṭiram, avaḷ pakkam pārāmal kaṭukaṭuttup pēcavum cīṟiviḻavun toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. avaṟṟai yellām avaḷ poṟumaiyē uruvāka niṉṟu tāṅki vantatum, avaṉ avaḷ mēluḷḷa veṟuppaic caṟṟum kuṟaikkāmal avaḷait tiṭṭi, “eṉ kuṭipperumaiyaiyum poruḷaiyum eṇṇittāṉē eṉ talaikkuk kaṇṇivaittāy. nī avaṟṟaiyē pōy maṇantu vāḻ” eṉṟu avaḷai rūsilāṉukkē aṉuppiṉāṉ. aṉuppi viṭṭup palanāṭukaḷilum tirintu iṟutiyil piḷāreṉcu eṉṉum nakaram vantu, annakar aracaṉatu paṭaiyiṟ cērntāṉ. vēṇṭā maṉaiviyuṭaṉ vāḻvatai viṭap pōril māḷvatē nallatu eṉṟa eṇṇattuṭaṉ, avaṉ vēṇṭumeṉṟē pōr muṉaikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu niṉṟu kālaṉai virumpi vaḻipāṭu ceytāṉ. āṉāl, ivvakaiyiṉāl avaṉukku, avaṉ virumpiya muṭivu ciṟitum kiṭṭavillai. nērmāṟāka avaṉ veṟṟiyum pukaḻum mikuntu paṭait talaivaṉ āvataṟkē aḵtu utaviṟṟu. 
rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi, mutalil, heleṉāvukku āṟutalāka, “perṭṭiram cīṟṟan taṇintu varuvāṉ; poṟuttiru,” eṉṟu kūṟivantāḷ. āṉāl, tiṅkaḷ oṉṟiraṇṭāyiṉa. varum vakai etuvuṅ kāṇōm. heleṉāvukku, rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭikkō, ulaka veṟuppup piṉṉum mikutiyāyiṟṟu.
innilaiyil, perṭṭiramiṭamiruntu kaṭitamoṉṟu vantatu. vaṟṟaṟpālaiyil nīr vēṭkaiyāṟ ceyaliḻantavaṉ, nīreṉṟa peyaraik kēṭṭavuṭaṉē uyirtteḻuvatu pōla, heleṉā uḷḷantuṭikka eḻuntu ceṉṟāḷ. rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭiyum ellām valla iṟaivaṉai vaṇaṅkik kaṭitattaip pirittup pārttāḷ. pārttavuṭaṉē avaḷ kaikaḷum uṭalum tuṭikka, “cī ciṟumaippaṭṭa patarē” eṉṟukūṟi nilattiṟ cāyntāḷ.
akkaṭitattil, “perṭṭiram maṇa nāḷiṟ koṇṭa veṟuppuc caṟṟum māṟātu. tāyē, heleṉāvukku uriyavaṉ nāṉallaṉ; avaḷ uṉṉaiyum uṉ kuṭiyaiyum poruḷaiyum virumpiṉāḷēyaṉṟi, eṉṉaiyō eṉ kātalaiyō virumpiṉavaḷ allaḷ; avaṟṟaiyē avaḷ aṭaika; nāṉ iṉi avaḷukkum uṉakkum uriya avvīṭṭil nuḻaiyēṉ,” eṉṟu eḻutiyiruntāṉ.
heleṉāviṉ uḷḷattil pālaip putarpōl ularntu niṉṟa kaikkiḷaik kātalaiyum ik kaṭitattaṉ tīmoḻikaḷ erittaṉa... “it tīyavaṉiṉ tīviṉaikkāka, vīṭṭukkuriyavar vīṭṭait tuṟappatā? vēṇṭām; nāṉē pōyviṭukiṟēṉ,” eṉṟu moḻintu avaḷ veḷi- yēṟiṉāḷ. rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi evvaḷavō taṭuttum, avaḷ puli kaṇṭu maruṇṭōṭupavaḷ pōl ōṭiṉāḷ.
2. mātar naṭpu
appoḻutu avaḷ uḷḷam paṭṭa pāṭṭai yārē kaṇittuk kūṟavallār? oru nēram, ‘kātalaṟṟa ivvulakiṉiṉṟu akaṉṟu viṭuvōmā’ eṉṟu eṇṇuvāḷ. āṉāl aṭutta nēram, ‘kātalaṉaṟṟa maṟu ulakil eṅṅaṉam ceṉṟu vāḻvēṉ’ eṉṟu eṇṇi alaikkaḻivāḷ. 
avaḷ maṉatil oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākap perṭṭiramiṉ vāḻkkaip paṭaṅkaḷ eḻunteḻuntu naṭaṉamiṭṭaṉa. ataṉ iṟutik kāṭciyai niṉaikka niṉaikka avaḷ aṅkaṅ kulaintatu, perṭṭiram aṉṟu veṟuppu niṟainta kaṇkaḷōṭu avaḷai ēḷaṉa nakaikoṇṭu nōkkip, “pētāy, eṉakkum uṉ kātalukkum ēṇi vaittālum eṭṭātu. itō, eṉ kaiyilirukkum ikkaṇaiyāḻiyai eṉṉiṭamiruntu, ettakait tiṟamiruntālum uṉṉāl vāṅka muṭiyumā? atupōlattāṉ eṉ kātalaip peṟum tiṟamum irukkum. nī ikkaṇaiyāḻiyaip peṟum nāḷē eṉ kātalaip peṟum nāḷ” eṉṟu cīṟiyuraitta kaṉal moḻikaḷ avaḷatu neñcattiraiyil koḻuntu viṭṭerintu tuṉpamākiya pukaiyai eḻuppiṉa.
eṉ ceyvāḷ pāvam! pañcu paṭappataikkum pātattaiyuṭaiya am melliyalāḷ, parukkaik kaṟkaḷeṉṟum paṭarmuḷḷeṉṟum paṭukareṉṟum pārāmal alaintu tirintu, iṟutiyil, viṉaip payaṉiṉ valiyāl, perṭṭiram vāḻntuvanta atē piḷāreṉsil vantu cērntāḷ.
ā, vitiyiṉ iyaipukiṭantavāṟu eṉṉē! heleṉāvai viṭṭoḻiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu veḷivanta perṭṭiramum, perṭṭiram vīṭṭilirātu akalavēṇṭu meṉṟu puṟappaṭṭa heleṉāvum ippaṭi ayal nāṭṭil vantoṉṟutal viyappuṭaiyataṉṟō?
heleṉā avvūril oru mūtāṭṭiyuṭaṉ taṅki avaḷ ātaravil iruntu vantāḷ. ammūtāṭṭiyiṉ putalvi 6tayāṉā eṉpavaḷ heleṉāviṉ maṟupatippō eṉṟu collampaṭi otta paruvamum otta uruvamum cāyalum uṭaiyavaḷāyiruntāḷ. ciṉṉāṭkaḷukkuḷ avviruvariṭaiyē iṇaipiriyā naṭpum paṟṟum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. appōtu avarkaḷ oruvarkkoruvar potuc ceytikaḷaiyum, piṉpu tattam viruppu veṟuppukaḷaiyum iṟutiyil tattam vāḻkkaic ceytikaḷaiyum kūṟik koṇṭaṉar.
avarkaḷ iruvarum, iru vēṟuṭalilum māṟip pukka orē uḷḷattiṉ iru pakutikaḷē eṉṉumpaṭi iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṉivuṭaṉ kalantu uṟavāṭi irukkum nāḷil, oru nāḷ heleṉā kalluṅ karaiyum vaṇṇam taṉ kātaṟ kataiyaiyum kātalaṉatu puṟakkaṇippiṉ pōkkaiyuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. kēṭṭa tayāṉā attaṉaiyum taṉakkē nikaḻntateṉat tuṭittut tattaḷittāḷ.
ellā vakaikaḷilum tayāṉā heleṉāvai muṟṟilum ottu iruntāḷ. āṉāl, helaṉā, kātaloḷiyaik kaṇṇuṟṟum ataṉ koṭiya vemmaiyuṭ paṭṭuk karuki vāṭiya malar pōṉṟavaḷ. tayāṉāvō, kātaliṉ kaṭaikkaṇippukkum puṟakkaṇippukkum oṉṟupōl āḷākāta naṟumalar pōṉṟavaḷ. heleṉāviṉ kataiyaik kēṭkak kēṭkat tayāṉāvukkut tuyar orupuṟam nēriṉum attakaiya tuyariṉum, heleṉāvait taṉ vaḻippaṭutta aṭimaiyākkum akkātal ettakaiya teṉṟum attakaiya kātalai avaḷiṭam eḻuppiya talaimakaṉ yāvaṉ eṉṟum aṟiyum vēṭkai ataṉiṉum mikuntu niṉṟatu.
avvēṭkai mikka tayāṉā, ataṟkēṟpat tāṉ avvakaiyiṟ kaṇṭa orē nikaḻccayai heleṉāvukku eṭuttuk kūṟiṉāṉ. uyar nilaiyiluḷḷavaṉum aḻaku mikkavaṉum maṅyair karuttai īrppavaṉumāṉa ōr iḷaiñaṉ, tāṉ veṟuttut taḷḷiṉum pōkāmal taṉṉait tuṉpuṟuttu vataiyum avaṉiṭamō piṟarevariṭamō taṉakku heleṉāviṉ kātalaip pōṉṟa uṇarcci eṟpaṭātataiyum avaḷ teḷinta uḷḷattōṭu eṭutturaittāṉ.
palanāḷ iṅṅaṉam uraiyāṭi varukaiyil iruvarum, tammil oruvaraip puṟakkaṇikkum kātalaṉaiyum, maṟṟoruvar puṟakkaṇippukku āḷākuṅ kātalaṉaiyum paṟṟip palavum pēcavar. appōtu heleṉā, avviruvaraip paṟṟiya uraikaḷum peritum ottirukka kaṇṭu viyantu orunāḷ tayāṉāviṭam avaḷai nāṭiya iḷaiñaṉiṉ peyar eṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ. avaḷ perṭṭiram eṉṟu kūṟiyatum, heleṉā tiṭukkiṭṭu, “eṉṉa, eṉṉa, perṭṭiramā? perṭṭiramā!” eṉṟāḷ.
heleṉāviṉ nilaikaṇṭu viyappuṟṟa tayāṉā, “eṉ ārūyirt tōḻī, ēṉ, appeyaril eṉṉa? ataṉāl uṉakku ēṉ ittaṉai paraparappu?” eṉṟāḷ.
“tayāṉā! iṉṉum avaraippaṟṟi caṟṟuk kūṟu. eṉ kātalaṉ peyarum atuvēyātalāl eṉ maṉam pataṟukiṉṟatu.”
“avar ētō rūsilāṉ kuṭiyaic cērntavar eṉṟu eṉṉiṭam kūṟiṉār.”
ivvurai kēṭṭatum heleṉā iṭiyēṟuṇṭa aravameṉa alaṟi vīḻntu, “ā, koṭumai koṭumai; kaṭavuḷē ituvum uṉ tiruvuḷḷamō?” eṉṟu pulampi mayaṅkiṉāḷ.
ippōtu, “an naṅkaiyar iruvar vāḻkkaiyaiyum iruvēṟu vakaiyiṟ pāḻpaṭutti varupavar, iruvēṟu maṉitarallar; oruvarē,” eṉpatu avarkaḷukku viḷaṅkiṟṟu. atumutal avarkaḷ perṭṭiramaippaṟṟi muṉpōl kaṉivuṭaṉ uraiyāṭuvatillai. āṉāl, heleṉāmaṭṭum taṉṉai veṟutta perṭṭiramai, avaṉ virumpaṉum tāṉ virumpātu āṭṭi vaikkum ittōḻi mūlamē taṉ vayappaṭuttalām eṉṟu karuti, akkaruttait tayāṉāviṟkut terivittāḷ. tayāṉāviṉ tāy perṭṭiramiṭan taṉ makaḷukkuḷḷa veṟuppaiyum, heleṉāviṉ tuyaraiyum kaṇṭu avarkaḷ ēṟpāṭṭiṟku iṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
3. paḻam naḻuvip pālil viḻuntatu
tayāṉā eṉṟum perṭṭiramai veṟuttu avaṉ kātalaip puṟakkaṇittē vantiruntāḷ. avaḷum avaḷ tāyum avaṉiṭam, “nīṅkaḷ periya iṭattaic cērntavarkaḷ. uṅkaḷuṭaṉ otta maṇa vāḻkkai koḷḷa eṅkaḷukkut takutiyillai,” eṉṟu teḷivākat taṅkaruttai aṟivittu miruntaṉar. avvāṟiruntum, kātaliṉ tūymaiyai muṟṟum aṟiyāta perṭṭiram, “maṇavāḻvu kiṭṭāviṭiṉuṅ kavalaiyillai; evvakaiyilum avaḷai aṭaintē tīruvēṉ,” eṉṟu taṉatu ciṟṟiṉpa vēṭkai tōṉṟak kūṟiṉāṉ.
kaṟpilum taṉ matippilum kuṟaivuṟāc celvarkaḷākiya tayāṉāvum avaḷ tāyum ittīmoḻikaḷ kēṭṭu neruppiṟ paṭṭa puḻuveṉat tuṭittuppoṅkiṉum, heleṉāviṉ naṉmaiyai eṇṇit tammai aṭakkikkoṇṭu avaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiṟku iṇaṅkiyavarkaḷ pōla naṭittu, avaṉait tayāṉā taṉiyē vantu kāṇuvataṟkāṉa iṭamum nēramum kuṟittaṉar. kuṟitta nērappaṭi kuṟitta iṭattil perṭṭiram vara, heleṉā tayāṉāviṉ tōṟṟattuṭaṉ aṅka vantu ārāk kātalōṭu avaṉuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāvi makiḻntāḷ. ammakiḻcci nērattil heleṉāviṉ kaṇaiyāḻiyaip perṭṭiram vāṅkik koṇṭāṉ. ataṟku māṟāka, avaṉ taṉa kaṇaiyāḻiyai heleṉāviṭam koṭuttāṉ.
avvaṇṇamē aracaṉ rūsilāṉ vantu rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭiyiṉ peruntuyarkaṇṭu taṉṉilai maṟantu kaṇṇīr ukuttuk kataṟiṉāṉ. piṉpu tiṟaṉuṭaiya tūtaraiyum oṟṟaraiyum ēvi, heleṉāvaiyum,perṭṭiramaiyum tēṭik koṇarumāṟu paṇittāṉ. avaṉ tūtarum oṟṟarum avarkaḷaip pala nāḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷiṉum tēṭiṉar. iṟutiyil avarkaḷ, perṭṭiramaip piḷāreṉcil kaṇṭu avvūr aracaṉ iṇakkam peṟṟu aṉaik koṇṭu vantaṉar. icceyti aṟinta heleṉā, tāṉum tayāṉāvaiyum avaḷ tāyaiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu nērē rūsilāṉ vantāḷ.
aracaṉ perṭṭiramiṭam heleṉāvaip paṟṟi ucāviya pōtu, avaḷ uyiruṭaṉ irukkiṟāḷō māṇṭoḻintāḷō taṉakkoṉṟun teriyāteṉṟum, avaḷ ituvaraiyil māṇṭirāviṭṭāl viraivil māḷavēṇṭumeṉṟē taṉ maṉam virumpukiṉṟateṉṟum, avaḷ māṇṭiruppiṉ, ataṉai aṟinta maṟunoṭiyē tāṉ iṉṉoru mātai maṇañ ceytu koḷḷumṭi ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉ teṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ. aracaṉ, “iṉṉoru māteṉṟatu yār?” eṉṟu kēṭkap perṭṭiram, tayāṉāvaip paṟṟi moḻintu, avaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ ēṟkeṉavē uḷamuvantu kaḷaviyal muṟaiyil maṇañ ceytu koṇṭatākavun terivittāṉ.
aracaṉ perṭṭiramiṭam ellaiyaṟṟa ciṉaṅ koṇṭā ṉāyiṉum, heloṉaviṉ ceytiyaṟiyātu etuvuṅ kūṟamuṭiyāta nilaimaiyiliruntāṉ.
appōtu, heleṉāvē muṉvaruvatu kaṇṭu, aracaṉ ellaiyaṟṟa makiḻcciyōṭu avaḷai etirkoṇṭu aḻaittāṉ. uṭaṉē avaḷaip perṭṭiramiṭam aḻaittuc ceṉṟu avaḷai ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi kūṟap perṭṭiram, tāṉ maṉam kalantu nēcikkum maṅkaiyaiyaṉṟi, vēṟu yāraiyum ēṟka muṭiyāteṉṟum, vēṇṭumāṉāl tāṉa virumpiya mātai maṇakkum uṟutiyuṭaṉ ivaḷ iṟakkum varaikkuṅ kāttirukkat tuṇintu viṭṭatākavum kūṟiṉāṉ.
aracaṉ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu, ‘iṉi ivaṉ iṟappatē naṉṟeṉṟu perṭṭiramai vīḻttapukum ellaiyil avaṉ kaiyiṟ ciṭanta kaṇaiyāḻi aracaṉ kaṇkaḷukkup pulappaṭṭatu. “itu nāṉ heleṉāvukkuk koṭutta kaṇaiyāḻi aṉṟō! iḵtu uṉ kaiyil eppaṭi vantatu? kūṟuka” eṉṟu kēṭka, avaṉ tikaittu miraḷa miraḷa viḻittāṉ.
annērattil heleṉā iṭaiyiṭṭup perṭṭiramiṭam taṉakku oru vaḻakkirukkiṉṟa teṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. heleṉā eṉṉa collap pōkiṟāḷ eṉṟu perṭṭiram avaḷ pakkam tirumpiyatum avaḷ taṉkaiyil aṇintirunta perṭṭiramiṉ kaṇaiyāḻiyai avaṉiṭam kāṭṭi, “itaṉai nāṉ peṟukiṉṟa nāḷē tam kātalaip peṟumnāḷ eṉṟu muṉpu moḻin tirukkiṉṟīr; avvāṟē, kaṇaiyāḻiyaip peṟṟēṉ; tam kātalaiyum peṟṟavāḷāṉēṉ; ippōtuyātu kūṟukiṉṟīr” eṉṟu tīrnta kuralil taṉatu vaḻakkai eṭutturaittāḷ.
perṭṭiram atu kēṭṭu, nikaḻntatu iṉṉateṉṟaṟiya iyalāmal tiṭukkiṭṭu, “itu nāṉ eṉ kātalikkaṉṟō koṭuttatu! nī tiruṭiyō, allatu kolai ceytōtāṉ itaṉaik kavarntirukka vēṇṭum; appaṭiyāyiṉ, uṉṉai eḷitil viṭēṉ.” eṉṟāṉ.
heleṉā, “aracē, ēḻaiyāyiṉum yāṉ tiruṭṭum, kolaiyum, koḷḷaiyum aṟiyēṉ; iccelvar eṉ uḷḷam koḷḷai koṇṭavar. pōtāteṉṟu eṉ tōḻi oruttiyiṉ kātalait tiruṭṭāṟ peṟavum, avaḷ kaṟpaik koḷḷai koḷḷavum muyaṉṟuḷḷār,” eṉṟu kūṟi, maṟaintu niṉṟa tayāṉāvaiyum avaḷ tāyaiyum veḷiyē varuvittu avarkaḷ mūlam kaṇavaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa kaḷaviyal maṇam avaṉ niṉaittapaṭi vēṟoru mātuṭaṉaṉṟu; taṉṉuṭaṉēyē nikaḻntatu eṉpatai viḷakkiṉāḷ.
perṭṭiram, heleṉāviṉ tiṟaṉaiyum vaṭamīṉ pōl caṟṟum māṟāta nilaiyāṉa kātalaiyuṅ kaṇṭu, tāṉ ceyta piḻaikaḷukkellām avaḷpāl maṉṉippuk kēṭṭu avaḷai maṉamāra ēṟṟukkoṇṭāṉ. tayāṉāviṉiṭamum taṉ piḻai poṟukkumāṟu vēṇṭi, avaḷait taṉa taṅkaiyākak karuti naṭattalāṉāṉ.
tayāṉāviṉ oppaṟṟa kaṟpuṟuti kaṇṭu viyanta maṉṉaṉ, heleṉāviṉ tiṟattukkup paricāka avaḷukkup perṭṭiramai aḷittatu pōl, tayāṉāvukku taṉ perumakkaḷuḷ oruvaraik kaṇavaṉākat tērnteṭuttu vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Goarard 
2.	Helena
3.	Rousillon 
4.	Bertram 
5.	Lafew  
6.	Diana

5. māṟāṭṭattāl nērnta pōrāṭṭam
Commedy of Errors
katai uṟuppiṉar
āṭavar:
1.ījiyaṉ: sairakkūs nakarc celvaṉ-iraṭṭaiyarāṉa iru antipōlaskaḷiṉ tantai.
2. mūtta antipōlas: ījiyaṉ mūtta makaṉ-epīsas nakarap paṭaittalaiṉ-atiriyāṉā kaṇavaṉ.
3. mūtta turōmiyō: mūtta antipōlasiṉ paṇiyāḷ-atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇip peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉ.
4. iḷaiya antipōlas: ījiyaṉ iḷaiya makaṉ-tāyaiyum tamaiyaṉaiyum tēṭa epīsas ntavaṉ.
5. iyai turōmiyō: iḷaiya antipōlasiṉ paṇiyāḷ-piṉṉāl atiriyāṉā paṇip peṇṇiṉ taṅkaiyai maṇantavaṉ.
6. epīsas nakarat talaivaṉ:
7. meṉapōṉ kōmakaṉ: nakarattalaivaṉ uṟaviṉaṉ-mūtta anti pōlasiṉ uyir naṇpaṉ-atiriyāṉāviṉ māmaṉ.
peṇṭir:
1. ījiyaṉ maṉaivi: (māṟṟuruvil) episasil maṭat talaivi-iru anti pōlaskaḷiṉ tāy.
2. atiriyāṉā: mūtta anti pōlasiṉ maṉaivi-meṉapōṉ kōmakaṉ maruki.
3. atiriyāṉāviṉ taṅkai: iṟutiyil iḷaiya antipōlasiṉ maṉaivi.
4. atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇippeṇ: mūtta turōmiyāviṉ maṉaivi.
5. atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇippeṇṇiṉ taṅkai: iṟutiyil iḷaiya turōmiyōviṉ maṉaivi.
6. naṭikai: mūtta antipōlasiṉ kātali.

kataic curukkam
kappaluṭaintataṉāl sairakkūs nakarac celvaṉ ījiyaṉuṭaiya mūtta makaṉ antipōlasum, avaṉ vēlaiyāḷ turōmiyōvum epīsasil taṅkiṉar. antipōlas nakarat talaivaṉ uṟaviṉaṉ meṉapōṉ naṭpiṉāl avaṉ marukiyākiya atiriyāṉāvai maṇantu paṭaittalaivaṉāy vāḻntāṉ. avaṉ paṇiyāḷ turōmiyōvum atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇip peṇṇai maṇantāṉ. ījiyaṉ maṉaivi atē nakaril oru maṭattil cērntu maṭat talaiviyāṉāḷ. ījiyaṉum mūtta anti pōlasuṭaṉ piṟantu avaṉaiyē pōṉṟa uruvuṭaiya iḷaiya antipōlasum, turōmiyōvaiyē pōṉṟa uruvuṭaiya avaṉ tampi iḷaiya turōmiyōvum sairak kūsil vāḻntaṉar. tamaiyaṉaiyum tāyaiyum tēṭi iḷaiya anti pōlasum turōmiyōvum epīsas cella, avarkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟic ceṉṟa ījiṉ sairakkūsarukketirāṉa epīsa! nakarattuc caṭṭap poṟiyuṭ paṭṭuk kōpat tīrppaḷikkap paṭṭāṉ. āyiṉum, avaṉ mītiraṅkit talaivaṉ mālai varai āyiram poṉ taṇṭam celuttit taṇṭaṉaiyiliruntu tappumāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. itaṟkiṭaiyil iru antipōlaskaḷum, iru turōmiyōkkaḷum orē uruvuṭaiyavarāyiruntataṉāl, atiriyāṉā, avaḷ paṇippeṇ mutaliyōrum, mūtta antipōlasiṉiṭam kātal koṇṭa naṭikaiyum avaḷ poṟ caṅkili ceyyak koṭuttirunta poṟkollaṉ oruvaṉum palavāṟu kuḻappamaṭaintaṉar. iṟutiyil ījiyaṉ tūkkiliṭap pōkum taṟuvāyil avaṉ maṉaiviyākiya maṭat talaiviyum, piḷḷaikaḷum oruṅkē vanta kuḻappamum taṟceyalāy nīṅka, aṉaivarum makiḻntaṉar. iḷaiya antipōlas atiriyāṉāviṉ taṅkaiyaiyum, iḷaiya turōmiyō avaḷ paṉippeṇṇiṉ taṅkaiyaiyum maṇantaṉar.
1. kuṭumpappirivu
1sairakkūs eṉṟa nakaril 2ījiyaṉ eṉṟu oru celvaṉ iruntāṉ. toḻilai oṭṭi, avaṉ cila kālam ayalnāṭu ceṉṟu vāḻavēṇṭiyiruntatu. appōtu avaṉukku, orē accil vārttavar pōṉṟa iraṇṭu putalvar piṟantaṉar. peyarilum vēṟṟumai illātapaṭi ījiyaṉ av viruvaraiyum 3antipōlas eṉṟē aḻaittāṉ.
apputalvarkaḷ piṟanta arē nērattil annakar viṭutiyil ōr ēḻai mātiṉukkum iraṇṭu piḷḷaikaḷ piṟantaṉar. avarkaḷum orē cāyalum tōṟṟamum uṭaiyavarā yiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ tāy avarkaḷukkum 4turōmiyō eṉṟu orē peyariṭṭu aḻaittāḷ. avaḷ mikavum ēḻai yāṉataṉāl avarkaḷait tāṉē vaḷarkka muṭiyāmal ījiyaṉiṭam oppaṭaittāḷ. ījiyaṉum avviruvaraiyum taṉ piḷḷaikaḷiṉ paṇiyāḷārāka vaittu vaḷarttu vantāṉ.
iru antipōlaskaḷum iru turōmiyōkkaḷum ciṟuvarāyiruntapōtu peṟṟōr, tam tāy nāṭṭukkup pōkap payaṇamāyiṉar. āṉāl kālakkēṭṭiṉāl, vaḻiyil avarkaḷ ēṟicceṉṟa kappal puyaliṟpaṭṭu noṟuṅki viṭṭatu. kappalil iruntavaruḷ oru cilar maṭṭumē paṭakukaḷil tappiṉar.

ījiyaṉum avaṉ maṉaiviyum tam piḷḷaikaḷ ovvoruvaraiyum avaravar paṇippiḷḷaikaḷuṭaṉē cērttu iraṇṭu marattuṇṭukaḷil kaṭṭi mitakka viṭṭārkaḷ. piṉ tāṅkaḷum taṉittaṉiyākap pāy marattil mitantu mitantu tappa muyaṉṟaṉar.
kāṟṟum alaiyum aṭikkum vaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu ellōrumē piḻaittaṉar. āyiṉum oruvar piḻaittatai iṉṉoruvar aṟiya muṭiyavillai.
ūḻvaliyāl iṅṅaṉam citaṟiya akkuṭumpattiṉaraip pala āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu atē ūḻvali 5epīsas eṉṉum nakaril vēṟu vēṟākak  koṇṭu vantu cērttatu. aṉaivarukkum piṟar uyir piḻaittatu teriyātatu pōlavē, aṉaivarum ōr iṭattil vantu cērntatum avarkaḷukkut teriyāmalē iruntatu. ataṉ payaṉāka avarkaḷ palavakaiyāṉa ikkaṭṭuk kaḷukkum kuḻappaṅkaḷukkum āḷāyiṉar.
avarkaḷ aṉaivarilum, mutaṉ mutalāka epīsas vantavar ījiyaṉ maṉaiviyum, mūtta antipōlasum avaṉ paṇiyāḷāṉa mūtta turōmiyōvumēyāvar. avarkaḷ annakarattuc cempaṭavar cilarāl kaṭaliliruntu kāppāṟṟap paṭṭaṉar. ciṟuvar iruvaraiyum tāyiṉiṭamiruntu pirittu accempavar piṟarkku aṭimaikaḷāka viṟṟuviṭṭaṉar.
makkaḷai iḻanta tāy maṉamuṭaintu, nakareṅkum pittuk koṇṭavaḷ pōl alaintāḷ. appōtu tuṟavi oruvar avaḷukku meyyaṟivu pukaṭṭa,  avaḷ oruvāṟu maṉanteḷintuṉuru maṭattil iṟaivaṉ paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭāḷ. avaḷ maṉat tūymaiyaik kaṇṭa maṭattut tuṟavimātar avaḷaiyē ammaṭattiṉ talaiviyākkiṉar.
ījiyaṉ makkaḷai vilaikku vāṅkiyavaṉ, nakart talaivaṉukku uṟaviṉaṉum peruvīraṉumākiya 6meṉēpōṉ kōmakaṉē. avaṉ mūtta antipōlasiṉ perumitat tōṟṟattaik kaṇṭu avaṉaip paṭaiyil oru paṇiyāḷaṉākac cērkkumpaṭi nakart talaivaṉait tūṇṭiṉāṉ.
paṭai vīrarāṉa antipōlas, taṉatu vīrattālum naṟkuṇattālum mikavum mēmpāṭeyti iṟutiyil, taṉakku utavi ceyta meṉepōṉ kōmakaṉ uyiraiyum kāppāṟṟiṉāṉ. ataṉ payaṉāka nakarttalaivaṉ avaṉaiyē taṉ paṭait talaivaṉāka amarttik koṇaṭāṉ. atumutal meṉēpōṉ kōmakaṉ avaṉaippiṉṉum arumaiyākap pārāṭṭit taṉ marukiyākiya 7atiriyāṉāvai avaṉukku maṇañ ceytu koṭuttāṉ.
antipōlasiṉ paṇiyāḷākiya turōmiyōvum atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇippeṇ oruttiyai virumpi maṇañ ceytukoṇṭu, avaḷuṭaṉēyē vāḻntu vantāṉ.
mūtta antipōlas iṅṅaṉam epīsasil nal vāḻvuṭaiyavaṉāyiruppatu sairakkūs nakarilirukkum avaṉ tantaiyākiya ījiyaṉukkum, iḷaiya antipōlasukkum teriyātu. ākavē avarkaḷ avaṉaip paṟṟik kavalaikoṇṭu avaṉai eṉṟa kāṇpōmō eṉṟu taṉittaṉiyāka ēṅkiyiruntaṉar.
pala āṇṭukaḷ iṅṅaṉam kaḻintum aṇṇaṉaip paṟṟiya ceyti etuvum teriyātatu kaṇṭu iḷaiya antipōlas tantaiyai aṇuki, “appā, iṉṉum nām vāḷā iruppatu naṉṟaṉṟu; nāṉāvatu pōy ammaiyaiyum aṇṇaṉaiyum tēṭi varukiṟēṉ” eṉṟāṉ.
ījiyaṉukkum uṇmaiyil maṉaiviyaip paṟṟiyum makaṉaip paṟṟiyum kavalai uṇṭu. āyiṉum, avarkaḷait tēṭat taṉ iḷaiya makaṉai aṉuppiṉāl eṅkē ivaṉaiyum iḻakka vēṇṭi vantuviṭumō eṉṟa añciṉāṉ. eṉiṉum, vēṟu vaḻiyiṉṟi avaṉ avarkaḷait tēṭumpaṭi iḷaiñaṉai aṉuppat tuṇintāṉ.

iḷaiya antipōlas iḷaiya turōmiyōvaiyum, uṭaṉkoṇṭu pala iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu taṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāṉaiyum nāyaiyum tēṭiyalaintu kaṭaiciyāka epīsas nakaram vantu cērntāṉ. annakarukkum sairakkūsiṟkum neṭunāḷ pakaimai uṇṭu eṉpatai avaṉ aṟivāṉatalāl. tāṉum paṇiyāḷum vēṟṟu nakarattārāka urumāṟi uṭceṉṟaṉar. ceṉṟu eṅkuñ cuṟṟi ōyvuṟṟu, aṅkuḷḷa viṭuti oṉṟil taṅki iḷaippāṟiṉar.
mutalil maṉaiviyaiyum oru piḷḷaiyaiyum iḻantataṉāl oru kaṇṇum kaiyum iḻantavaṉ pōṉṟirunta ījiyaṉ, eñciya kaṇṇum kaiyumāy amainta iḷaiya makaṉaiyum piriya nērntatiṉāl mikavum maṉamuṭaintāṉ. āṉāl, eppaṭiyum avviḷaiyōṉ muṉ iḻanta maṉaiviyaiyum makaṉaiyum tēṭi aḻaittuk koṇṭu varuvāṉ eṉṟa nampikkaiyil ījiyaṉ cilakālam kaḻittāṉ. nāḷāka āka an nampikkaiyum maṟaiyalāyiṟṟu. iṟutiyil avaṉ poṟumai iḻantu, mutaliṟ kāṇāmaṟ pōṉavaraiyum piṉpu avarkaḷait tēṭic ceṉṟavaraiyum cērttut tāṉē ceṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭippateṉat tuṇintu puṟappaṭṭāṉ.
2. ījiyaṉ tuyar
iḷaiya antipōlasai epīsas pakkam iḻuttuc ceṉṟa atē ūḻ, ījiyaṉaiyum annakariṉ pakkamē koṇṭu vantu cērttatu.
epīsasukkum sairakkūsiṟkum iṭaiyil pakaimai uṇṭu eṉpatai ījiyaṉ maṟantu sairakkūsar uṭaiyilēyē nakaril pukuntu viṭṭāṉ. ākavē nakark kāvalar avaṉaip piṭittukkoṇṭu pōy nakart talaivaṉ muṉ niṟuttiṉar.
talaivaṉ īraneñciṉaṉāyiṉum naṭunilai piṟaḻātavaṉ. ātalāl ījiyiṉiṉ āṇṭu mutircciyaik kaṇṭu parivuṟṟāṉāyiṉum, nakarc caṭṭattait tavirkkak kūṭāmal āyiram veḷḷi taṇṭa vari celutta vēṇṭum; aṉṟēl avaṉ talaiyiḻattal vēṇṭumeṉat tīrppaḷittāṉ.
ījiyaṉ taṉ tuyaramikka varalāṟu muṟṟum nakart talaivaṉukku eṭutturaittāṉ. atukēṭṭu maṉamiraṅkiya attalaivaṉ, taṇṭaṉaiyai niṟaivēṟṟa mālai varaiyil tavaṇai koṭukkak kūṭumeṉṟum, ataṟkuḷ nakaril naṇpar evarēṉum iruntāṟ kaṇṭupiṭittu āyiram veḷḷiyaik koṭuttu uyir piḻaikkalāmeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ.
ījiyaṉ ittīrppuk kēṭṭu, aṉṟē taṉ uyir akaṉṟateṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. āyiṉum, mālaivaraiyil nēramirukkiṉṟatu. orukāl ataṟkuḷ iṟaiyaruḷ ētēṉum vaḻikāṭṭutaluṅ kūṭum eṉṟa nampikkaiyuṭaṉ cuṟṟic cuṟṟi alaintāṉ. mālai aṇuka aṇuka acciṟu nampikkaiyum vaṟaṇṭu pōyiṟṟu.
kaṟuttiruṇṭa mukiliṉiṭaiyilum veḷḷiya miṉṉaṟ koṭi tōṉṟi oḷirvatupōla, nampikkaiyaṟṟut tuyar cūḻnta iṭattilum maṟaintu niṉṟaḷikkum iṟaiyaruḷ oḷi vīcum eṉpatai yārē aṟivar! ījiyaṉ tāṉ āḷaṟṟut tuṇaiyaṟṟut tavippatāka niṉaittu maṉamaḻuṅkiya atē nāḷil, aṇmaiyilēyē avaṉ maṉaiviyum avaṉ piḷḷaikaḷum iruntaṉar eṉpatu avaṉukkut teriyātaṉṟō? atilum, iṟaiyaruḷāl oru makaṉ niṟaicelvamum uyarnilaiyum uṭaiyavaṉāy arukiluḷḷa toru māḷikaiyil vīṟṟiruntāṉ eṉpatai eppaṭi avaṉ aṟiyakkūṭum?
itaṟkiṭaiyil iṅṅaṉam, orē vaṭivuṭaiya iraṇṭu antipōlaskaḷum iraṇṭu turōmiyōkkaḷum annakaril iruntatu avarkaḷiṭaiyē palavakaiyāṉa maruṭcikaḷaiyum, kuḻappaṅkaḷaiyum uṇṭu paṇṇiṟṟu.
3. kuḻappaṅkaḷ
iḷaiya antipōlas taṉ paṇiyāḷiṭam paṇappaiyaik koṭuttuk, kaṭaikkup pōyc camaiyalukkāṉa poruḷkaḷ vāṅkivara aṉuppiviṭṭut tāṉ nakar pārkkum eṇṇattuṭaṉ terukkaḷil cuṟṟik koṇṭiruntāṉ.
mūtta antipōlas atē nērattil taṉatu paṭai nilaiyattil iruntāṉ. uṇavu nēramākiyum toḻil mikutiyāl aṉṟu avaṉ vīṭu cella muṭiyāmal kālantāḻtta nērntatu. avaṉ maṉaiviyākiya atiriyāṉa akkālat tāḻvukkuk kāraṇan teriyāmal palavakaiyil taṉmaṉattai araṭṭalāṉāḷ. 
avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉiṭam māṟāp paṟṟu vāyntavaḷāyiṉum, aiya maṉappāṉmaiyāl alaikkaḻiyum neñciṉaḷāyiruntāḷ. itaṉāl avaḷ avaṉukku nēram vēḷai eṉpatiṉṟi eppōtum tontaravu koṭuttu vantāḷ. aṉṟaikku muntiṉa nāḷum avaḷ aṅṅaṉam tontaravu ceyta pōtu antipōlas, “ippaṭi nī eṉṉai eṉṟum arippatāyiṉ, nāṉ uṉṉai viṭṭuviṭṭu vēṟu maṉaivitāṉ pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum” eṉṟu ciṉantu kūṟiṉāṉ.
veḷḷaiyaṟiviṉaḷākiya atiriyāṉā, avaṉ mēlīṭākak kūṟiya icciṉa moḻikaḷai appaṭiyē meyyeṉak koṇṭu maṉamuḷaintāḷ. iṉṟu, ataṟkēṟpa, antipōlas vēḷaiyil vīṭṭukku varak kālan tāḻttatu kaṇṭu, avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉ taṉṉiṭam coṉṉapaṭiyētāṉ taṉṉai viṭṭu viṭṭāṉō eṉṟu mikka accaṅkoṇṭāḷ. eṉavē, avaḷ taṉ paṇiyāḷākiya mūtta turōmiyōvai aḻaittut taṉ kaṇavaṉait tēṭikkoṇṭu varumpaṭi aṉuppiṉāḷ.
turōmiyō taṉ talaivaṉait tēṭikkoṇṭu pōkumpōtu, vaḻiyil iḷaiya antipārasaik kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṭu avaṉ taṉ talaivaṉē eṉṟu eṇṇiya turōmiyō, talaivi aḻaikkiṉṟāḷ eṉṟu kūṟi vīṭṭukku iḻuttāṉ. āṉāl, avvanti polas, itturōmiyōvait taṉ paṇiyāḷākiya turōmiyōvē eṉak karuti vekuḷiyuṭaṉ, “eṉṉiṭam ēṉ nakaiyāṭukiṟāy? eṉakkut talaiviyumillai, kolaiviyumillai; ēṉ tontaravu ceykiṟāy?” eṉṟu cīṟiṉāṉ. turōmiyō pōkāmal piṉṉum pacuppuvatu kaṇṭu, avaṉai antipōlas naiyappuṭait taṉuppiṉāṉ.
turōmiyō aḻutukoṇṭu talaiviyiṉiṭam vantu, “ammaṇi, taṅkaḷ kaṇavar, nāṉ pōy aḻaittatum kaṭuñciṉaṅ koṇṭu eṉṉai aṭittut turattiviṭṭār. mēlum, avar ‘eṉakkut talaiviyumillai, kolaiyumillai’ eṉṟu veṟutturaikkiṟār” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. ataik kēṭṭatum, atiriyāṉāviṉ maṉakkuḻappam muṉṉaiyiṉum paṉmaṭaṅku mikutiyāyiṟṟu. uṇarccic cuḻaliṟpaṭṭa avaḷ, maṉam pataittut taṉ kaṇavaṉaic cīṟṟan taṇittu aḻaittu varat tāṉē puṟappaṭṭāḷ.
turōmiyō kūṟiya kuṟippaip piṉpaṟṟi atiriyāṉā oru paṇip peṇṇuṭaṉ iḷaiya antipōlas irukkum viṭutikku vantu cērntāḷ. turōmiyōvaip pōlavē avaḷum avaṉait taṉ kaṇavaṉ antipōlasē eṉṟa niṉaittāḷ. ākavē, avaḷ avaṉait taṉṉuṭaṉ vīṭṭukku vantu uṇavaruntumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉāḷ. antipōlas uṇmaiyil avaḷ yār eṉṟē aṟiyātavaṉātaliṉ, oṉṟum viḷaṅkātu viḻittāṉ. caṟṟu muṉṉatākap paṇiyāḷ kūṟiya moḻikaḷaik kūṭa, avaṉ, ‘vēṇṭumeṉṟē ēḷaṉamākak kūṟiya puṉaicuruṭṭu’ eṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. ippōtu avaṉ kataikku ēṟṟapaṭi tāḷamiṭa, ippeṇ eṅkiruntu vantāḷ eṉṟu avaṉ niṉaittu viyappaṭaintāṉ.
āṉāl, avaṉ evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu atiriyāṉāvi ṉiṭam ētilaṉāka otuṅki naṭantu koṇṭāṉō, avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu avaḷ avaṉiṭam kaṇavaṉ eṉṟu urimai koṇṭāṭik keñci vaṟpuṟutti aḻaikkalāṉāḷ. am moḻikaḷai maṟukka muṭiyāmaiyiṉālum, paci vēḷaiyā yiruntataṉālum, ippittalāṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ muṭivutāṉ eṉṉa eṉṟu terintukoḷḷum viruppattiṉālum avaṉ avaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi avaḷ illattai aṭaintāṉ.
atiriyāṉāvukkut taṅkai orutti iruntāḷ. avaḷum atiriyāṉāip pōlavē aḻakumikkavaḷ. āṉāl avaḷiṭam atiriyāṉāviṉ paṭapaṭappō aiyamaṉappāṉmaiyō ciṟitum illai. atiriyāṉā antipōlasai vaṟpuṟutti uṇavaḷikkum pōtellām, avaḷ caṟṟē akala niṉṟiruntāḷ. atiriyāṉāviṉ kaṭumoḻikaḷaiviṭa, avaḷuṭaiya kalaṅkiya iru viḻikaḷum, avvappōtu kūṟum aṉpu niṟainta arumoḻikaḷum antipōlasiṉ maṉattai mikutiyāka iyakkiṉa. avaṉ taṉvayamaṟṟavaṉāy avarkaḷuṭaṉ iṉṉuraiyāṭi aṉpōṭu uṇṇalāṉāṉ.
4. kaṇavaṉ ēmāṟṟam
itaṉiṭaiyil atiriyāṉāviṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa maṟṟaiya antipōlas, maṟṟa nāḷiṉum mikutiyāka nērañceṉṟu vīṭṭiṟkut tirumpiṉāṉ. varumvaḻiyil taṉṉait tēṭivarum paṇiyāḷ turōmiyōvaik kaṇṭu avaṉaiyum aḻaittuk koṇṭu vantāṉ.  āṉāl iruvarum vīṭu vantu cērntapoḻutu vīṭṭiṉ vāyil aṭaittiruntatu. vīṭṭiṉuḷḷiruntu, atiriyāṉāvum avaḷ taṅkaiyum yāruṭaṉō aḷavaḷāvip pēcuṅkural kēṭṭatu. veḷiyil katavait taṭṭiyatu avarkaḷukkuk kēṭkavillai. 
āṉāl, katavarukil irunta paṇippeṇkaḷ cilar, ‘yār aṅkē’ eṉṟu kēṭṭārkaḷ iruvarum tām iṉṉār eṉpatait terivitta aḷavil avarkaḷuḷ orutti kolleṉa nakaittu, “ēṉaiyā, ippaṭi aṭāppoy aḷakkiṟīr! talaivarum eṉ kaṇavarākiya avar paṇiyāḷum uḷḷiruntu uṇṭu koṇṭirukka, avarkaḷ peyarāl eṉṉai ēmāṟṟukiṟīrē’ eṉṟu eṭutteṟintu kūṟi ēḷaṉañ ceytāḷ. atukēṭṭu mūtta antipōlas, kaṭuñ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu taṉ nilaiyattaiyē nōkki mīṇṭāṉ.
avaṉ pōkum vaḻiyil oru naṭikaiyiṉ vīṭu iruntatu. avaṉ cila nēraṅkaḷil poḻutu pōkkiṟkāka aṅkē ceṉṟu avaḷuṭaṉ pēciyiruppatuṇṭu. iṉṟu taṉ maṉaivi taṉṉai avamatittatai eṇṇi maṉam veṟuppuṟṟu, avaḷiṭam ceṉṟu caṟṟup pēcik kaḷittirukkalām eṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. avaḷum avaṉ varaviṉ arumaiyaṟintu pōṟṟi, avaṉiṭam parivāy uraiyāṭiṉāḷ. atukaṇṭu kaḷippuṟṟa antipōlas, tāṉ taṉ maṉaikkeṉṟu ceytuvarum poṟcaṅkili oṉṟai avaḷukkut taruvatāka vākkaḷittāṉ. avaḷum avaṉ parivukku makiḻntu, taṉ kaik kaṇaiyāḻiyai eṭuttu avaṉ kaiyil aṇivittāḷ.
poṟcaṅkiliyaic ceyya ēṟṟukkoṇṭa poṟ kollaṉ. atu muṭintatum antipōlasaik kāṇa vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ. vaḻiyil, avaṉ iḷaiya antipōlasaik kaṇṭu, avaṉē mūtta antipōlas eṉṟu niṉaittu avaṉiṭam caṅkiliyaik koṭuttāṉ antipōlas atu taṉataṉṟu eṉṟa pōtu, avaṉ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu “ippōtu eṉataṉṟu eṉpataṉāl eṉṉa payaṉ? umakkeṉac ceytāyiṟṟu; iṉi nīr atai ēṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatē” eṉṟu atai  avaṉiṭam eṟintu viṭṭup pōṉāṉ.
koñcanēraṅ kaḻittu appoṟkollaṉ oru kaṭaṉ kāraṉāl ciṟai piṭikkappaṭṭāṉ. kāvaluṭaṉ ciṟaikkūṭattiṟkuc cellum vaḻiyil, avaṉ mūtta antipōlasaik kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṭavuṭaṉ caṅkilikkup paṇam koṭukkumpaṭi avaṉaik kēṭka, antipōlas, “paṇam irukkaṭṭum, caṅkili eṅkē?” eṉṟāṉ. poṟkollaṉ, “ummiṭantāṉ caṟṟumuṉ koṭuttēṉē” eṉṟu tuṇivākak kūṟiṉāṉ. iruvarum vātāṭiṉār kāvalar, iruvaraiyum ciṟaippiṭittu nakarttalaivaṉiṭam aḻaittēkiṉar.
iṅṅaṉam ciṟai cellutal, taṉ uyarnilaikkut takāteṉa antipōlas eṇṇiṉāṉ. ākavē tāṉ vaḻiyil kaṇṭa iḷaiya turōmiyōvait taṉ paṇiyāḷ eṉṟu niṉaittut taṉ maṉaiviyiṭam ceṉṟu tāṉ viṭutalai peṟuvataṟkāṉa poruḷai uṭaṉē vāṅkivarap paṇittāṉ.
turōmiyō annērattil uṇmaiyil taṉ talaivaṉākiya iḷaiya antipōlasait tēṭikkoṇṭu vantāṉ. ēṉeṉṟāl, avarkaḷ itaṟkumuṉ, ‘pittar pōṉṟu naṭakkum makkaḷaiyuṭaiya immāya nakarai viṭṭu uṭaṉē nīṅkavēṇṭum’ eṉṟu uṟuti ceytiruntaṉar. ātalāl, iḷaiya antipōlas, kappal puṟappaṭum nērattai aṟintu varumpaṭi taṉ turōmiyōvai veḷiyē aṉuppiyiruntāṉ. kappal oṉṟu aṉṟē puṟappaṭa iruntatai aṟinta turōmiyō. atai aṟivikkattāṉ taṉ talaivaṉ antipōlasai nāṭi vantāṉ. āṉāl taṉ ēṟpāṭukaḷukku ovvāta moḻikaḷai avaṉ ippōtu pakarntatu kēṭṭu turōmiyō, perituṅ kuḻappamuṟṟāṉ.
āyiṉum, ‘paṇiyāḷukkuriya kaṭaṉ kāriya kāraṇa ārāycciyaṉṟu; kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi naṭappatēyām’ eṉat tuṇintu, avvantipōlasaik kaṇavaṉ eṉṟu urimai pārāṭṭiya atiriyāṉāviṉ vīṭu ceṉṟu nilaimaiyait terivittup paṇam kēṭṭāṉ. kaṇavaṉiṭam ellaiyaṟṟa paṟṟu vaittirunta atiriyāṉā, uṭaṉē pataittuk koṇṭu tāṉē paṇattaik koṭuttaṉuppiṉāḷ.
turōmiyō appaṇattaik koṇṭu celkaiyil iḷaiya antipōlasaik kāṇavē, avaṉ ‘nam talaivaṉ itaṟkumuṉ kāvaliliruntāṉē; eppaṭi veḷivantāṉ’ eṉṟa viyappuṭaṉ paṇattai avaṉmuṉ vaittāṉ. paṇam ētu eṉṟu antipōlas kēṭka, avaṉ, “nīṅkaḷ kēṭṭuviṭṭapaṭiyē uṅkaḷaik kaṇavar eṉṟaḻaitta ammātu tāṉ koṭuttāḷ” eṉṟāṉ.
caṟṟumuṉ oru poṟkollaṉ oru caṅkiliyaik koṭuttuc ceṉṟatum, taṉ paṇiyāḷē muṉpiṉ muraṇāka naṭappatum kāṇa, iḷaiya antipōlasiṉ mukattil ‘annakar ētō cūṉiyakkāriyiṉ mantira valaiyiṟpaṭṭa māyanakar pōlum’ eṉṟa aiyam valuttatu.
innilaiyil mūtta antipōlasuṭaṉ naṭpup pūṇṭa naṭikai aṅku vantu, taṉakkuc ceyta uṟutippaṭi caṅkili varātataṉāl taṉkaṇaiyāḻiyait tiruppik koṭuttuviṭum paṭi avaṉai vēṇṭiṉāḷ.
iḷaiya antipōlas, “nī yār eṉpataiyē nāṉ aṟiyēṉ, āyiṉum, ētō caṅkili eṉṟu nī kūṟuvataṟkēṟpa oruvaṉ iccaṅkiliyai eṉṉiṭam eṟintu pōṉāṉ” eṉṟu colli ataṉai avaḷiṭam koṭuttāṉ.
avaḷ caṅkiliyai eṭuttukkoṇṭu vīṭṭukkup pōkumvaḻiyil atiriyāṉāvaik kaṇṭu, tāṉ aṟinta varalāṟukaḷellām colli, antipōlasukkuk kaṭṭāyam pittuttāṉ piṭittirukka vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu taṉ karuttai veḷiyiṭṭāḷ.
5. āṭavar nalivu
atiriyāṉāvum taṉṉiṭam avaṉ noṭikku oruvakaiyāka naṭappatai ōrntu avaḷ kūṟuvatu uṇmaiyāka irukkakkūṭum eṉṟu nampiṉāḷ. appōtu iḷaiya antipōlasum avaṉuṭaṉ pūcaliṭṭa vaṇṇamāka iḷaiya turōmiyōvum vantaṉar. tam kaṇavar pittuk koṇṭaṉar eṉṟeṇṇiya atiriyāṉāvum avaḷ paṇippeṇṇum avarkaḷiṭam naṭikaiyiṉ ceytiyaik kūṟi, avarkaḷai uṇmaiyilēyē veḷikoḷḷac ceytaṉar. eppaṭiyāvatu tappiṉāl pōtum eṉṟu avarkaḷ timiṟik koṇṭōṭap  pārkkavē, peṇṭir avarkaḷ pittarātalāl aṭaittu vaippatu naṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭu avarkaḷaip piṭikkac ceṉṟaṉar. āṭavar iruvarum ciṉantu eḻuntaṉarāyiṉum attakaiya nerukkaṭiyil vēṟu vakaiyaṟiyāmal ōṭicceṉṟu pakkattilirunta oru periya kaṭṭiṭattiṉuḷ nuḻaintaṉar. atiriyāṉāvum paṇippeṇṇum aṅkum nuḻaiya muyaṉṟaṉar.
akkaṭṭiṭamē ījiyaṉ maṉaivi maṭattalaiviyāka amarntu naṭattiya maṭamēyākum. iḷaiya antipōlasum iḷaiya turōmiyōvum atiriyāṉāvil turattappaṭṭut tañcam, tañcam eṉat taṉ maṭattiṟ pukuntapōtu, avaḷ, avarkaḷ taṉ piḷḷaiyum paṇiyāḷumēyāvar eṉpatai aṟiyātavaḷāyt taṉ iyaṟkai irakka maṉappāṉmaiyiṉāl avarkaḷukkut tuṇai tara muṉvantāḷ.
atiriyāṉavum avaḷ paṇippeṇṇum tam kaṇavar pittuk koṇṭavarātalāl, avarait tammiṭamē oppaṭaikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉar. āṉāl, maṭattalaiviyāṉa ījiyaṉ  maṉaivi, “avarkaḷ aṟiviḻintu pittuk koṇṭāl, nīṅkaḷ amaiti iḻantu pittuk koṇṭirukkiṟīrkaḷ; avarkaḷai ātarikkum nilai uṅkaḷukkillai. nāṉē avarkaḷai ātarittu uṅkaḷiṭam oppuvikkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭāḷ.
mēlum, atiriyāṉā taṉ kaṇavaṉiṭam maṭṭaṟṟa paṟṟuk koṇṭavaḷāyiṉum, ataṉ kāraṇamākap poṟumaiyum nalluṇarvum iḻantu, avaṉ mītu aiyaṅkoṇṭu avaṉukku ōyāta tontaravu koṭuttu varuvataiyum maṭattalaivi uyttuṇarntu koṇṭāḷ. ākavē, avaḷ taṉatu iṉiya nēriya moḻikaḷāl atiriyāṉavukku avaḷ kuṟṟam teṉpaṭum vaṇṇam aṟivurai nalkiṉāḷ. atiriyāṉavum teruṭciyuṟṟup poṟumaiyuṭaṉ kaṇavaṉ kuṟippaṟintu naṭappatāka ottuk koṇṭāḷ. āyiṉum, maṭattalaivi avaḷai muṟṟum nampi antipōlasaiyum turōmiyōvaiyum avaḷiṭam oppaṭaikka maṉamillāmal avarkaḷ aṉavairaiyum nakarttalaivaṉiṭam koṇṭu pōy niṟutti avaṉ mūlam āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉāḷ.
6. muṭivu
antipōlasiṉ tāy maṭattalaiviyāy irunta immaṭattiṉ muṉpuṟamē annakariṉ tūkkumēṭai amaintiruntatu. mālai varaiyil naṇpar evaraiyuṅ kāṇāmaiyāl taṇṭavari celutta vakaiyillāta ījiyaṉaik kāvalar kolait taṇṭaṉaikkāka ammēṭaikkuḷ koṇṭu vantaṉar. avaṉuṭaṉ, attaṇṭaṉaiyai vēṇṭā veṟuppāka niṟaivēṟṟum poṟuppai ēṟṟu nakarat talaivaṉum vantāṉ.
itē nērattil, ciṟaiyil vaikkappaṭṭa mūtta antipōlas taṉ uyar nilaiyiṉ utaviyāl ciṟaik kāvalaṉai accuṟutti vilakki viṭṭut turōmiyōvuṭaṉ attalaivaṉ muṉ vantu, tamakku nakaril nērnta palavakai iṉṉalkaḷaiyuṅ kūṟi, ‘ivaiyaṉaittum yārō etirikaḷiṉ mayāmāyirukka vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu maṉṟāṭiṉāṉ. talaivaṉ, aḵtu iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyātu kuḻappamuṟṟāḷ.
ūḻvaliyāl ittaṟuvāyil, maṭattalaiviyāka ījiyaṉ maṉaivi, iḷaiya antipōlasaiyum iḷaiya turōmiyōvaiyum atiriyāṉāvuṭaṉum paṇippeṇṇuṭaṉum kūṭṭik koṇṭu vantu taṉ maṭattiṉ muṉ naṭantavai aṉaittum pukaṉṟāḷ. itaṟkuḷ, atiriyāṉāvum avaḷ paṇippeṇṇum tattam kaṇavar uruvuṭaṉ oruvarukkiruvar niṟpatu kaṇṭu ellaiyillā viyappum viḻippum aṭaintaṉar. nakart talaivaṉum avarkaḷaik kaṇṭu, “iḵteṉṉa, ivarkaḷ vaṭivum, ataṉ niḻalum pōla ottu niṟkiṉṟaṉarē; ivarkaḷ iruvarō oruvarō” eṉa malaivuṟṟāṉ.
appōtu tirumaṭat talaiviyiṉ uḷḷattil oru pēroḷi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. avaḷ iru antipōlaskaḷaiyum vāri eṭuttu, “ā, nīṅkaḷ eṉ piḷḷaikaḷ allirō” eṉṟu aḻutāḷ. ījiyaṉum taṉ tuyaramellām maṟantu tāṉē piḷḷaikaḷiṉ tantai eṉak kūṟik koṇṭu ōṭivantu, arukiruntu iṉpak kaṇṇīr ukuttāṉ.
maṭattalaivi, iru antipōlaskaḷum orē uruvuṭaiya tam iru piḷḷaikaḷ eṉṟum. uṭaṉ irukkum oru turōmiyōkkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē paṇimātu orutti peṟṟa iraṭṭaip piḷḷaikaḷ āvar eṉṟum kūṟi, ījiyaṉ taṉṉai viṭṭu neṭunāṭ pirintirunta taṉ kaṇavaṉē eṉṟum, tām pirintukūṭiya ivviyattaku varalāṟu muṟṟum eṭutturaittāḷ.
atukēṭṭu aṉaivarum ellaiyilā viyappum makiḻcciyum eytiṉar.
iḷaiya antipōlas atiriyāṉāviṉ taṅkaiyaiyum, iḷaiya turōmiyō avaḷ paṇippeṇṇiṉ taṅkaiyaiyum virumpi maṇantaṉar.
nakart talaivaṉ iḷaiya antipōlasait taṉ karuvūlat talaivaṉ ākkiṉāṉ. aṉaivarum iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntu vantaṉar.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Syracuse
2.	Aegeon
3.	Antipolis
4.	Dromio 
5.	Ephesus
6.	Duke of Menophon  
7.	Adriana
eee




cēkcupiyar kataikaḷ - 4
mutaṟ patippu - 1945
innūl 2002 il ēḻumalai patippakam, ceṉṉai - 88. veḷiyiṭṭa patippai mūlamākak koṇṭu veḷivarukiṟatu.
1 - nāṉkām heṉri
(ḻanacēla aiē)
katai uṟuppiṉar
āṭavar:
1.nāṉkām heṉṟi aracaṉ: riccarṭukkup piṉ avaṉai vilakki aracaṉāṉavaṉ. riccarṭiṉ ciṟṟappaṉāṉa jāṉ āpkāṇṭiṉ makaṉ.
2.heṉri iḷavaracaṉ allatu hāl : nāṉkām heṉri aracaṉuṭaiya mūttamakaṉ. iḷamaiyil ḵpālsṭāḵp kūṭṭattāruṭaṉ iṉpa vāḻvilīṭupaṭṭavaṉ. 
3. jāṉ iḷavaracaṉ : heṉri iḷavaracaṉ tampi; nāṉkām heṉriyiṉ iḷaiya makaṉ.
4. ōveṉ kiḷeṉṭōvar : vēls talaivaṉ.
5.eṭmaṉṭ mārṭṭimar:  mārc iḷaṅkōvaṉ - riccarṭukkuc ciṟṟappaṉ makaṉ nāṉkām heṉriyiṉ periyappaṉ makaṉ-riccarṭiṟkup piṉ varaṉmuṟaippaṭi aracukku urimaiyuṭaiyavaṉ- vēls talaivaṉ ōveṉ kiḷeṉṭōvar makaḷai maṇantavaṉ.
6.nārtamparlantuk kōmakaṉ : riccarṭu aracaṉukketirāka nāṉkām heṉṟikku utavi ceytu, avaṉukkum etirākat tirumpiyavaṉ - heṉripersi allatu hāṭspariṉ tantai.
7. heṉri persi allatu hāṭspar : peruvīraṉ, aṭaṅkāt taṟutalai - skāṭlantiliruntu paṭaittalaivaṉ ṭakḷasaic ciṟaip piṭittu viṭutalai ceytu naṭpākkik kiḷarcciyil cērttuk koṇṭavaṉ. heṉri iḷavaracaṉuṭaṉ otta akavaiyuṭaiyavaṉ- avaṉ etiri.
8.ṭakḷas : skāṭlantup paṭaittalaivaṉ.
9.sarjāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵp: vīḻcciyaṟṟa peruṅkuṭiyāḷaṉ-oppaṟṟa nakaiccuvaiyuṭaiyavaṉ-iḷavaracaṉ kīḻnilait tōḻaruṭ ciṟantavaṉ.
10.kārlail talaimakaṉ : riccarṭṭiṉ teyvīka urimaiyai vaṟpuṟutti nāṉkām heṉriyāl kolaiyuṇṭavaṉ.
11.pārṭālp : sarjāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵpiṉ tōḻaruḷ oruvaṉ.
12.sar vālṭṭar piḷaṉṭ: aracar pakkattu niṉṟu ṣrūsparīc caṇṭaiyiṉ muṉtūtaṉākac ceṉṟavaṉ.
13. vorsṭar: kiḷarccikkārar pakkattilirunta srūs pariccaṇṭaiyiṉ muṉ tūtarākac ceṉṟavarkaḷ.
14. veṭarṉaṉ:
15. pāyiṉs : sar jāṉ ḵpāslṭāḵp tōḻaṉ.
16.sarjāṉ : pālsṭāḵpiṉ vēṟu tōḻaṉ.
17.taṇṭattalaivaṇaṉ.
18.yārk perun talaimakaṉ : iraṇṭām kiḷarcciyait talaivaruḷ oruvaṉ.
19. mēprē : iraṇṭām kiḷarccit talaivaruḷ oruvaṉ.
20. tiruvāḷar  poḷḷal : sar jāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵp cērtta cottaippaṭai vīrarkaḷ.
21. tiruvāḷar vāymūṭi:
22. hēsṭiṅs : iraṇṭām kiḷarccit talaivaruḷ oruvaṉ
23. vesṭu mōrlantu : iraṇṭām kiḷarccik kālattil jāṉ iḷavaracaṉuṭaṉ irunta talaivaṉ.
24. vārvik perumakaṉ: aracavaiyiluḷḷa perumakkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ.
25.sar heṉri kāskāyiṉ: sar jāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵpai veṟutta vaḻakku maṉṟat talaivaṉ.
kataiccurukkam
nāṉkām heṉri aracaṉ taṉakku muṉ irunta riccarṭu aracaṉai vīḻtti aracaṉāṉavaṉ. riccarṭu aracaṉiṭam poṟāmai koṇṭa perumakkaḷ ātaraviṉālum, nalāṭcimuṟaikaḷāl peṟṟa potumakkaḷ ātaraviṉālumē avaṉ aracaṉāka muṭintatu. avaṉukku uḷnāṭṭilum veḷināṭṭilum etirikaḷ palar. vaṭakkē skāṭlāntil ṭakḷas eṉṟa paṭaittalaivaṉum mēṟkē vēls talaivaṉ ōveṉ kiḷeṉṭōvarum avaṉukku aṭaṅkātavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ. atōṭu avviraṇ ṭellaiyilum uḷḷa iraṇṭu peruṅkuṭiyiṉarum avaṉukku muṉpōl uṭpakaiyāyiṉar. avaruḷ vēls ellaiyilirunta mārc kōmāṉ eṭmaṉṭ mārṭṭimar riccarṭiṟkup piṉ varaṉmuṟaippaṭi aracaṉāka vēṇṭiyavaṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ skāṭlāntu ellaiyiluḷḷa nārtamparlantup perumakaṉ. ivaṉ heṉrikku aracaṉāka utaviyavaṉ. ivaṉ makaṉ heṉri persi eṉṟa hāṭpasparukku mārṭṭimariṉ taṅkaiyai maṇampurivittataṉ mūlam ivaṉ mārṭṭimar naṭpaip peṟṟāṉ. atōṭu mārṭṭimar vēls talaivaṉāl ciṟaip paṭuttap paṭṭapōtu avaṉ putalviyaik kātalittu maṇantāṉ. skāṭlāntup paṭaittalaivaṉ ṭakḷasum iṅkilāntu mītu paṭaiyeṭuttuka hāṭsparāl ciṟaip piṭikkap paṭṭāṉ. aracaṉ avaṉait taṉ kaiyil oppaṭaikkumāṟu kēṭṭataṟku hāṭspar mārṭṭimarai mīṭka vaṟpuṟuttiṉāṉ. aracaṉ ataṟku iṇaṅkāmaiyāl ṭakḷasiṟku viṭutalai tantu avaṉaiyum naṇpaṉākkiṉāṉ. iṅṅaṉam ellā etirikaḷum oṉṟu cērntu kiḷarcci ceytu ṣrūspari eṉṉumiṭattil aracaṉai etirkkak kāttiruntaṉar.
hāṭsparuṭaṉ otta akavaiyuṭaiya heṉri iḷavaracaṉ. avaṉukku nērmāṟāyk kīḻmakkaḷ kūṭṭuṟavai virumpi avarkaḷuṭaṉ kīḻāṉa viṭutikaḷil cuṟṟi alaintāṉ. avaṉuṭaṉ tōḻarā iruntavaruḷ nakaiyaracaṉāṉa sarjāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵpum oruvaṉ. kāṭshil eṉṟa iṭattil avarkaḷ naṭattiya ciṟu tiruṭṭil kūṭa avaṉ uṭaṉ kalantu kāvalaruṭaiyil ḵpālsṭāḵpai ēḷaṉam ceytāṉ. āṉāl, tantaik ketirāṉa kiḷarcci paṟṟik kēṭṭatum avaṉ kuṇam māṟi ḵpālsṭāḵp mutaliya palarutaviyāl periya nāṭṭup paṭaiyait tiraṭṭik koṇṭu ṣrūspari ceṉṟāṉ. kiḷarccikārariṭaiyil kōlaittaṉattāl nārtamparlantu vilaki niṟka, hāṭspariṉ muṉ ciṉattāl kiḷeṉṭōvarum vilaki niṉṟāṉ. kiḷeṉṭōvariṉ marumakaṉākiya mārṭṭimarum vēṇṭāveṟuppākac caṇṭai ceytāṉ. kiḷarccikkārar tōṟṟaṉar. hāṭspar heṉri iḷavaracaṉ kaiyāl iṟantāṉ. ḵpālsṭāḵp iṟanta hāṭsparait tūkkic ceṉṟu taṉ kaivēlai eṉṟu kūṟik kēli nāṭakam naṭittāṉ.
eppakkam veṉṟālum ataṉuṭaṉ cērvateṉṟu kāttu niṉṟa nārtamparlantu makaṉ iṟanta ceyti kēṭṭut tikaittāṉ. yārk talaimakaṉ tōṟṟuvikkum iraṇṭām kiḷarcciyil cērntu paḻivāṅka vēṇṭumeṉṟa eṇṇattait tuyarāl maṉamuṟinta avaṉ maṉaiviyum putalviyum taṭukka, iraṇṭām muṟaiyum avaṉ kiḷarccikkārarai ēmāṟavaittāṉ. heṉri iḷavaracaṉ vēls talaivaṉai aṭakkac ceṉṟiruntāl jāṉ iḷavaracaṉ aracar paṭaittalaimai tāṅkiṉāṉ. avaṉ tūtar mūlam kiḷarccip paṭaiyōr kuṟaikaḷai akaṟṟuvatākak kūṟi avarkaḷaik kalaittāṉ. iḷavaracaṉuṭaṉ tuṇait talaivaṉāyirunta vesṭu mōrlantiṉ cūḻccittiṟattāl iḷavaracaṉ paṭaimaṭṭum kalaiyātiruntatu. kiḷarccit talaivar ēmāntu ciṟaippaṭṭaṉar.
mutumaiyālum tuyarkaḷālum piṇiyālum nalivuṟṟu maṉṉaṉ paṭukkaiyil kiṭantāṉ. avaṉ cōrvaip piriveṉṟeṇṇi iḷavaracaṉ tuyarum, taṉakkup poṟuppu vantuṟṟatē eṉṟa eṇṇamum unta, muṭiyuṭaṉ kaṭavuḷai vaṇaṅkac ceṉṟiruntāṉ. ataṟkiṭaiyē viḻitteḻunta maṉṉaṉ ataṟkuḷ muṭiyaik kavara avaṉ viraintāṉ eṉṟeṇṇi aiyamum ciṉamum koṇṭāṉ. piṉ avaṉ heṉri iḷavaracaṉ coṟkaḷāl uṇmai yuṇarntu makiḻvuṭaṉ  uyirnīttāṉ.
iḷavaracaṉ heṉri taṉ muṉṉaik kuṇam maṟantu putu maṉitaṉāy iṅkilāntiṉ oppaṟṟa aracaṉāṉāṉ. naṟkuṇamuṭaiyavaṉāyiṉum, aracaṉait taṉ  vaḻiyilirukka muyaṉṟa ḵpālḵṭāpḵp kūṭṭattiṉar puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭaṉar.  avarkaḷai iḷavaracaṉuṭaṉ caṭṭappaṭi taṇṭitta sarheṉrikāskāyiṉ eṉṟa taṇṭattalaivaṉ naṉku matittup pōṟṟap paṭṭāṉ.
1. tuṇaikkaruvikaḷē pakaikkaruvikaḷāṉatu
 piṟappurimaiyāl aracarākātu kuṭikaḷ iṇakkam peṟṟu aracirukkai ēṟiya āṅkila maṉṉaruḷ1 nāṉkām heṉri aracaṉ oruvaṉ.
avaṉukkumuṉ āṇṭa2  iraṇṭām riccarṭu maṉṉaṉukku avaṉ ciṟṟappaṉ makaṉ. āṉāl, avaṉ tantaikkum mūttavaṉāṉa iṉṉoru ciṟṟappaṉuṭaiya makaṉ oruvaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ peyar3 eṭmaṉṭu mārṭimar. aracurimai oḻuṅkuppaṭi avaṉē aracaṉāy vantirukka vēṇṭum. āṉāl, riccarṭiṉ taṉṉāṇmai āṭciyaip perumakkaḷ etirttapōtu, heṉritāṉ avarkaḷukkut talaimai pūṇṭāṉ. eṉavē, riccarṭu muṭi tuṟantapōtu avaṉē apperumakkaḷiṉ iṇakkattālum tuṇaiyālum taṉṉai aracaṉākkik koḷḷa muṭintatu.
taṉṉalattiṉ vayappaṭṭu riccarṭiṉ pōkkaik kaṇṭittē heṉri aracaṉāṉāṉ. āyiṉum aṅṅaṉam aracaṉāṉapiṉ avaṉum anta riccarṭaip pōlavē, taṉṉāṇmaiyuṭaṉ aracāḷat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. appōtu perumakkaḷ, riccarṭai veṟuttatataiviṭa mikutiyāka avaṉai veṟukkat toṭaṅkiṉar. “riccarṭāvatu muṟaippaṭi aracaṉāyiruntavaṉ; appaṭiyallaṉē ivaṉ; ivaṉ maṇimuṭi nam kaippaṭak koṭutta koṭaitāṉē; ivaṉukkeṉṉa ittaṉai taṉṉāṇmai?” eṉṟu avarkaḷ cīṟiṉar.
mēlum riccarṭiṉ taṉṉāṇmaikku avaṉ iṉpaviruppum cōmpal vāḻvumē kāraṇam. taṉṉalamaṉṟu. aracaṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyilallātu taṉimaṉitaṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyil avaṉ naṟkuṇamum pīṭum perun taṉmaiyum uṭaiyavaṉ. heṉriyō itaṟku māṟākat taṉṉalamum cūḻcciyum uṭaiyavaṉ. eṭutta kāriyattai niṟaivēṟṟum vakaiyil avaṉ ettakaiya muṟaiyaiyum kaiyāḷat tayaṅkātavaṉ. avaṉ kaiyil aracurimai vantatē. avaṉtāṉ riccarṭiṉ koṭumaiyōṭukūṭa avaṉiṭam illāta ciṟumaiyum uṭaiyavaṉ eṉṟu kāṭṭikkoṇṭāṉ.
heṉri, riccarṭai akaṟṟittāṉē aracaṉākap pōkum nāṭkaḷil avaṉai aracavaiyil etirttuniṉṟu, “aracurimai teyvīkamākavum piṟappurimaippaṭiyum varuvatu; teyvattālaṉṟi maṉitar evarālum aḵtu eṭukkappaṭavō koṭukkappaṭavō kūṭiyataṉṟu” eṉṟu 1kārlail talaimakaṉ kūṟiyiruntāṉ. ataṉ payaṉāka heṉri avaṉait tūkkiliṭa nērntatu. camayattuṟaiyiṉuḷ talaimaiyāṉa oruvaṉ. naṭunilai urimai kūṟiyataṟkāka uyiriḻantatu kaṇṭu, accamayat tuṟaiyiluḷḷa talaimakkaḷ ellām piṟarum heṉriyiṭam pakaimai koḷḷalāyiṉar.
